# Campaign Chronicle - Gatefall: Pendragon

**Campaign:** Gatefall: Pendragon - **World:** Gatefall
**Ledger:** Canonical Record (`011` Section 8) preserving campaign events in durable narrative form. It holds the campaign's Events and their narrative record.

---

# This save (`EVT-001219`-`EVT-001225`): the cafeteria and the flight; the Shattered City, Level 211 (GB-63's XP corrected); the Glasswalker carried out; the menders and the lot unsold; the Director's memorandum; the EU ruling and three notifications.
# Prior save (`EVT-001213`-`EVT-001218`): the pool, the sauna and the morning; Kane's briefing on the Director; London tunnelled and its filing returned; 38 A-Rank pieces bought and handed to Anjali; a standing instruction.
# Prior save (`EVT-001207`-`EVT-001212`): GB-63's Thralls and Warden, Level 211; the loot and the strip; the filing to Renata and the 13:00 lot; home -- Kane and Dario, the shower, Dario's disclosure; the Director moved to tonight.
# Prior save (`EVT-001195`-`EVT-001206`): the Jiu Valley fence -- Andrei, the 211 names, Viorica and Gheorghe, five young awakened; four branches live; the 10-01 tick; Central Street; the kitchen -- Renata, Seoul, Europe; the 65th daily; GB-63 and Level 210.
# Prior save (`EVT-001180`-`EVT-001194`): the roster sent and London's lock; the regulators, Milwaukee and Toledo dispatched; Region V -- Boyce and a signed statement, Farraday and the annex, Director Okafor, the acceptance; Chicago on the 06:00 rotation; five contract Gates cleared; Kesha's map and the tunnel floor; Kane and Dario, the training plan; the gear backlog and $5.39M of C/B gear; the 64th daily fails, the Hive penalty zone, Level 209; the families write.
# Prior save (`EVT-001169`-`EVT-001179`): the far door opened and Orenne's slates, the reads; the owner ruling that a champion may cross to Earth; Orenne crosses and the door closes; the cities (Prague, London, Chicago, Tel Aviv, Abisko); Orenne killed in the cage room, Succession (eleven transcribed, six raised, three declined) and its rulings; the body burned and the remains stored; home, the sauna; Anjali's call -- nine A-Rank crews and their commanders (ENT-000344-ENT-000351), the A-Rank registry ruling; the working Rank revoked; promotion barrier.
# Earlier per-save notes: sealed (Decision 094), see `sealed/`.
# 2026-09-24, out of play, at the player's direction: first seal pass (Decision 094). No Event moved; no canonical value changed except where a stale value was brought up to what its own cited Events establish. `ENT-000136` is Priya Menon throughout (her former surname, Okafor, collided with `ENT-000107`); ruling-provenance asides were stripped from live text with every Event citation kept. ## EVT-001001 - Ten Self-Opened S-Rank Instant Dungeons

```yaml
id: EVT-001001
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-15T11:00:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-08"
type: Event
kind: combat
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-15 11:00-13:00 -05:00 (approx.)"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
counter_deltas:
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.rupture.successful_uses, delta: 13 }
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: record-updated
    record: ENT-000125
description: "**Ordinary combat only — ten trivial S-Rank clears, ordinary attacks/movement only, no material skill credit beyond routine kill resolution.** Ten S-Rank instant-dungeon keys bought from the System shop, 900,000 g each (-9,000,000 g, gold 300,356,081 → 291,356,081), and opened back to back at the Winnetka house. Every clear resolved as a trivial stomp (effective 100, net +4 modifier steps from Stat margin, equipped titles, and weapon mastery) — no damage taken across all ten.

  **Population and archetypes (all real rolls):** Key 1 — 11 commons, 2 elites, 1 boss, Overgrown Temple (bonus loot). Key 2 — 10 commons, 1 elite, 2 bosses, Beast Den. Key 3 — 10 commons, 1 elite, 1 boss, Ashfield. Key 4 — 8 commons, 1 elite, 1 boss, Ashfield. Key 5 — 13 commons, 3 elites, 1 boss, Overgrown Temple (bonus loot). Key 6 — 21 commons, 0 elites, 1 boss, Hive. Key 7 — 3 commons, 2 elites, 1 boss, Flooded Mine. Key 8 — 10 commons, 0 elites, 1 boss, Hive. Key 9 — 9 commons, 2 elites, 1 boss, Ashfield. Key 10 — 5 commons, 2 elites, 2 bosses, Beast Den.

  **XP: 208,000** (commons/elites/bosses at S-Rank kill value, ×2/×4, plus one 3,200 milestone per instance; no underdog bonus, creature Rank = System Rank). **Level 186 → 197** (eleven crossings; level 190's crossing ran a Title Assessment, nothing new qualified). Final: **Level 197, XP 7,642/19,700.** Past level 100 the rung is +1 to all five Stats per level (+11 each) plus +5 unspent points per level (+55; unspent 17 → 72, unallocated as of this Event). Health/Mana restored to new maxima at each crossing.

  ```text
  Base      2919 / 2644 / 2898 / 2645 / 3126
  Effective 2955 / 2700 / 2910 / 2645 / 3147
  Max Health 11,640 (full)   Max Mana 6,294 (full)
  ```

  **Loot, banked, none sold or equipped this Event:** 3,584 mined-deposit S-Rank crystals + 100 per-kill S-Rank crystals (3,684 total), 26 S-Rank cores (174 → 200). Boss drops — 6 equipment (a second Arcanist Torso [S], a second Bastion Torso [E, boss-imprinted], two Longswords [S], a Mindspun Loop [S], a Guard Shield [S]), 4 duplicate Skill Runes (all already-known starting skills, banked), 1 unopened Skill Book (logged for review), 2 potion caches (3 Mana Potions; 1 Healing + 2 Mana Potions, both from the two Overgrown Temple bonus loot rolls)."
```

## EVT-001002 - Kane's Evening; BGM's Terms, the Guild Pitched, a Board Seat Accepted

```yaml
id: EVT-001002
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-15T19:00:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-08"
type: Event
kind: relationship-turning-point
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-15 19:00-late -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000242
counter_deltas: []
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Elias Kane visited the Winnetka house that evening as planned, beer in hand. Disclosed BGM National's offer: a Field Liaison reclassification off the federal-asset roster, attached to an unofficial 'coordination visibility' reporting line on Alexander specifically (nothing official ties them together, but Kane's proximity to the field's newest carded name was enough for BGM to want a designated point of contact). Alexander advised him to counter with full autonomy and no reporting line, or no deal — Kane agreed to draft exactly that counter that night.

  **Alexander disclosed `OBJ-60`'s guild plan in full for the first time to anyone** and asked Kane to sit an independent board seat — full vote, able to overrule him, no guild membership required, no obligation beyond the seat itself. **Kane accepted.** Alexander named he would offer Tereza Blažková a seat in person, Thursday, Prague; Kane is the first person told of the plan at all.

  Evening continued with drinks (the standing joke: Alexander's Vitality makes him functionally unable to get drunk). Both stayed the night; physically intimate again, mutual, no change to either party's read of the friendship. `REL-000107` updated."
```

## EVT-001003 - The 2026-09-16 06:00 World Tick

```yaml
id: EVT-001003
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-16T06:00:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-08"
type: Event
kind: world-tick
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-16T06:00:00-05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
counter_deltas: []
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "**`quiet_days` 3 → 4** — 2026-09-15 read as a quiet day (ten instant dungeons are content beneath his tier; nothing else that day was a genuinely contested scene). The crucible floor at 4 owes one guaranteed on-route audition (doubled anomaly, worse result kept) and one guaranteed 8.4.2-qualifying crisis within reach, ahead of the ordinary roll.

  **Chicago rolls 6 Gates (tier VII, `1d4+5`), Prague rolls 4 (`1d2+2`).** Ranks/siting, real rolls: Chicago — E (elsewhere), C (elsewhere), B (within reach), A (elsewhere), B (within reach), C (within reach, reassigned on-route by the crucible floor, no Gate sited naturally on-route). Prague — D, C, E, D, all elsewhere.

  **Forced audition:** the reassigned Chicago C-Rank posting. True Rank read from Combat Tier on first entry: S (🎲 3, 01–70 band). Anomaly rolled twice per the doubled-anomaly rule, worse kept: **red gate** both times (🎲 67, 🎲 51). Population: Hive archetype (🎲 2) — 20 commons, 0 elites, 1 boss, S-Rank, sealed on entry. **Never entered this span.**

  **Sited crisis:** the other Chicago B-Rank, within reach — a containment cordon failed, population already loose where people are. **Never reached or answered this span**; not routed to Alexander by BGM (his standing arrangement covers confirmed high-Rank emergencies, and a B-Rank break is ordinary regional response work). Counts as an active break for escalation-credit purposes; the world's own crews are presumed responding off-screen.

  **Board:** `GB-38` posted (Chicago, unconfirmed B-Rank, within reach, deadline midnight 2026-09-20). `GB-37` remains live from the prior span. **52nd daily quest issues, streak eligibility 40 pending completion.**"
```

## EVT-001006 - Dario and Tereza Confirmed; BGM's Office of Incident Review Calls

id: EVT-001006

description: "Texted confirmations exchanged with Dario Wexler (tonight, 20:00, The Sidecut — unchanged) and Tereza Blažková (Thursday 19:00, her chosen bar, Reinhardt and Aubrac both confirmed).

  **`OBJ-58` moves for the first time since it opened.** A call arrived from **BGM National's Office of Incident Review**, on behalf of a joint national/international coordination panel, regarding the Jiu Valley platform-access record naming Alexander and Sorcha Bellweather. Alexander gave a deliberately minimal, deflecting account — present, cleared, no observed anomaly, first visit to the site — declining to confirm or deny anything about the Exclusion's actual end. The reviewer noted the account as 'careful' but had no contrary evidence to press against it; logged the statement as given, made no escalation, left the channel open for future contact. **Institutional half: moved and stalled in the same conversation — logged, not escalated, not closed.**"
```

  Alexander pitched the independent S-Rank network and, separately, the Pendragon Guild — plain, hunter-first, no answer to any guild, government, or country — and asked her to sit an independent board seat: full vote, no guild membership required, no obligation beyond the seat. She tested him directly on whether the vote was real ('what happens the day the board tells you no on something you want'); Alexander answered honestly that he could be outvoted. **She accepted the seat.**

  Invited to Thursday's Prague meeting; accepted, contingent on Rift-step transport (confirmed). Pickup arranged: his door, 10:45 Mountain tomorrow (2026-09-17), for the 19:00 Prague meeting. Lunch continued informally — Kane and Alexander's Prague teleport-demonstration story retold, Alexander's concealment (an established, previously undisclosed-to-Marisol capability) demonstrated briefly for comic effect. `REL-000116` minted."

  - **`EVT-001007`** (Tolo/Orenne circuit) — Rift-step uses unchanged (no anchored step this Event). Eleven candidate results, six ratified.

  - **`EVT-001010`** (Palos Preserves) — Rift-step +2 uses (Palos Preserves, Kane's apartment). No combat.
  - **`EVT-001011`** (Sevrin/Ilith/Aumry circuit) — Rift-step uses unchanged (no anchored step this Event). Eighteen candidate results, eleven ratified.

  **Reconciled totals.** Health: **11,596/11,596 → 11,792/11,792** (Vitality allocation only; no combat, no damage, all session). Mana: **418/6,272 → 3,870/6,370** (full breakdown itemized per-Event above and in `100_CHARACTER_SHEET.md`'s `mana_note`). `skills.rift_step.successful_uses` **102 → 104** (Palos Preserves, Kane's apartment; instant-dungeon keys and far-door crossings do not consume Rift-step charges).

  **`gatefall.skill_formation`: tracking.** Seventeen ratifications this span (Read the Hand, Quiet House, Arithmetic, Patience of the Ledger, Weight of Precedent, Nothing Is Wasted, Every Seam He Has Walked, Mapwork, Grip on the Rail, Refuse the Opening, Weight of the Span, The Question He Waits On, Fourfold Step, Sightline, Hold Together, Quartermaster, Set the Line). ## EVT-001014 - Standfast, Weaponized: A Real Redirect Branch

```yaml
id: EVT-001014

description: "**Kane attempted, at Alexander's request, to voluntarily collapse Standfast aimed at a target rather than waiting for an attacker to break it — a genuinely unprecedented use of the field, consented to and controlled throughout.**

  Four attempts, real rolls: (1) 🎲 5, effective 5 — fizzle, field collapsed inert, nothing discharged. (2) 🎲 85, effective 85, slow/deliberate — strong success, a controlled pulse landed on Alexander, felt but harmless at his scale. (3) 🎲 35, net +1 (technique known), effective 55, deliberately overcharged with half his pool (Mana 2,300 → 1,150) — partial success, a ragged, harder hit: **Health 11,792 → 10,974 (-818)**. (4) 🎲 67, net +1, effective 87, slow approach + heavy Mana (900/1,150 committed, Mana → 250) + genuine intent — strong success, a focused, aimed strike: **Health 10,974 → 10,156 (-818 again)**. Both hits read as roughly A-Rank-devastating force by the Section 6.2 fixed-hunter formula, trivial only against Alexander's own scale. Both healed in full overnight via ordinary Section 6.1.1 recovery before the next morning.

  **Standfast now carries a genuine voluntary-discharge branch, natively Kane's own — a second exception to Section 13.6's signature-fixity rule, alongside the forced-redirect branch from `EVT-000952`.** Provisional, pending a real profile ruling on how the two branches relate. Kane assigned informal ongoing practice ('homework') toward a one-shot A-Rank-lethal threshold; no fixed schedule set."
```

Her actual wait, given Mountain being only one hour behind Central, ran to minutes rather than the near-hour first assumed.

  All four arrived in Prague at 19:00 local — exactly meeting time, the seven-hour Chicago/Prague offset landing noon Central precisely on 19:00 CEST, no slack before Tereza Blažková, Lukas Reinhardt, and Camille Aubrac arrived.

  Reinhardt (Brandhof, world-ranked #8) and Aubrac (Lumière Guild, world-ranked #6) met Alexander in person for the first time. Reinhardt characterized his own signature, **Bastion** — a summoned, rigid fortification, holds ground by not failing rather than by moving. Alexander disclosed his teleportation capability, including passenger transport, to both — the fifth and sixth confirmed holders (after Owen, Kane, Tereza, Dario). Reaction: genuine recalibration from both, no request for mechanism detail. Alexander also disclosed that Kane's own Standfast had been reshaped from purely defensive to carrying a genuine offensive branch, and offered the same reshaping to the table generally — both Reinhardt and Aubrac expressed real, if cautious, interest without committing on the spot.

  **The Pendragon Guild pitched to both.** Reinhardt accepted a provisional independent board seat, conditional on it staying advisory rather than becoming a second employer. Aubrac accepted provisionally as well, reserving the right to withdraw if it read as image management rather than real judgment. **The board now stands at five: Kane, Cade, Blažková, Reinhardt, Aubrac** — two more than Kesha Morrison's own 'minimum viable' three."

He named directly that he'd noticed Alexander's restraint the prior night — not pressing the Jiu Valley question in front of the table — and admitted he'd already concluded, unprompted, that Alexander was the one who ended the Exclusion: **the timeline, the scale, and Alexander's own lack of reaction to Cade's 'held a line on that thing' all read as confirmation to him.** He did not ask for it to be said aloud. Alexander disclosed, unprompted, that his own parents died in the Damen Avenue Break at fifteen years old — the first time this fact has been told to anyone outside campaign-established history — offering it as the reason he understood Reinhardt's own reluctance to discuss the Jiu. **Alexander offered to confirm the Jiu Valley kill directly, but flagged the café as the wrong venue given Brandhof's own foot traffic; both agreed to revisit it somewhere quieter, un-dated.** Reinhardt's own read of Alexander sharpened considerably by the exchange — genuine curiosity extended rather than merely professional interest."
```

  **Renata's read, unfiltered.** National scope is the easier ask — nothing bars a charter from declaring national reach at founding, and given the board's own geography, chartering narrow would be false modesty. Speed is the harder one: she can signal institutional weight behind the filing, which buys attention rather than a rubber stamp, but cannot compel Region V's own review pace. On whether a guild could charter through BGM National directly rather than any region at all, she was honest that she didn't know — no national charter authority has ever existed in the regulation because no founder has ever been positioned to test the gap before, and she preferred to ask quietly rather than guess and have it fail later. Alexander named plainly, without meaning it as leverage, that an unworkable US path left founding the guild entirely outside US jurisdiction as a real, standing option — Renata read the honesty in it and chose to frame the same fact upstairs as 'genuine founder optionality' rather than a threat, judging correctly that repeating it as an ultimatum would read wrong and cost more than it bought.

  Both threads left open and tracked: Kesha pushing the ordinary Region V charter as fast as it will go; Renata quietly checking whether national-scope-from-founding is real, untested ground."

  Alexander texted Tereza Blažková for a professional film crew that could shoot him the same day, quietly, offering double their ordinary rate for the rush. **Tereza, unasking as always ('double AND quiet. ok I'm not going to ask'), sent one name: Sanne Verhoeven** (`ENT-000283`), a small Amsterdam crew who shoots what the CGA doesn't want seen and Horizon doesn't want overheard, flown same-day for Tereza twice — and warned she won't pick up an unknown Chicago number without Tereza's name. Asked where/when, the piece's length, and stated her one non-negotiable — paper, not handshake quiet: every hand that touches the footage signs an NDA with six-figure liquidated damages per breach, editors included. Alexander asked for full discretion until the piece officially airs and offered to come to her. **Her terms: her own base near Schiphol, full control of the rig; rush plus quiet plus same-day is her premium tier — €80,000, cash or wire, before rolling; room ready in ninety minutes.** Alexander: 'I'll be there in 90 minutes.'

All four were player-caught and re-narrated the same turn; none is canon."

## EVT-001035 - One Take, Clean

```yaml
id: EVT-001035
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-20T16:25:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-11"
type: Event
kind: social
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-20 ~16:25-17:00 -05:00 (23:25-24:00 +02:00), Sanne Verhoeven's studio, Amstelveen"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000283
counter_deltas: []
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000283
    result: record-updated
    record: REL-000119
description: "A converted light-industrial unit off a residential street, no sign, a keypad and a camera that read him before he knocked. Sanne — thirties, functional, a nod instead of a handshake — with a serious three-camera soundproofed rig and two techs already running cable. **Papers first: the NDA signed by Alexander, Sanne, both camera techs and the editor**, six-figure liquidated damages per breach. She never asked what the secrets were.

  Her five questions (format, length, who airs it, one take or redos, kill switch) and Alexander's answers: no kill switch, no broadcaster signed yet — he'll hand it over later — a message to people, pitching his guild; he asked for her recommendation. **Her read:** no desk and flag; five minutes at most; a sit-down to camera in her controlled room for the sensitive part, said once, cut against a second half of silent footage of him actually doing the work somewhere real, under a tighter re-cut of the same audio.

  **The statement, one take, verbatim as spoken to camera:**

  > My name is Alexander Pendragon. I'm System Rank S. I'm going to say some things once, and then I'm going to tell you what I'm building.
  > Jiu Valley has been a UN exclusion zone since 2020. Most of you know it as a line on a map you're told not to cross. What you weren't told is what was actually down there — something that had already killed six S-Ranks before I was ever asked to look at it. Three trainees died on a rescue attempt nobody talks about. Thousands of people lost everything the valley used to be, for six years, because the people who were supposed to tell you the truth decided silence was easier to manage than the truth was.
  > Two weeks ago I went in. Alone. Off a platform I wasn't officially standing on, for an agency that still won't confirm I was there. It took under two minutes. It's dead. I checked. Every instrument on that platform read the signature go quiet, and then absolutely nobody filed anything, because admitting it existed meant admitting six years of silence had a name attached to it.
  > I'm not telling you this to be a hero. I did it because nobody else was going to, and because I was in a position to just do it instead of asking permission first. I'm telling you now because you deserved to know before I decided to say it on television, and because I'm done letting an office with a letterhead decide what you're allowed to hear about your own lives.
  > So here's what I'm building. The Pendragon Guild. And here's what it actually means, not what it sounds like.
  > Every hunter. Every Rank. I don't care if you've cleared one Gate or a thousand — if you're doing this work, you have a place in this Guild. No velvet rope, no minimum Rank to get through the door.
  > And it's not only hunters. Artificers who keep gear alive when a supply chain won't. Medics who've kept somebody breathing on the wrong side of a Gate with nothing but what fit in a bag. This isn't a roster of people who fight. It's people who look out for each other, in whatever way they're built to do it. If that's you, you're already in.
  > You will never run a Gate understrength again. Every Gate gets crewed to its Rank — the right number of people, the right Rank of people, checked before anyone steps through, not after someone doesn't come back. That's not a courtesy. That's the floor.
  > You get real training. Not a binder and a pat on the back — actual time with people who know more than you do, for as long as it takes. The people teaching you sit on my board. Every one of them S-Rank, every one of them still doing the work, not just judging it from above. That's not a guest lecture. That's who's actually building this with me.
  > You get guild gear. If you don't own what the job needs, we lend it to you. Nobody clears a Gate under-equipped because they couldn't afford to survive it.
  > And you get to talk to me. Not a spokesperson, not a form, not a queue — me. If something's wrong, you say it, and it gets answered. That's the whole system. It's not complicated. It's just something nobody's bothered to actually do.
  > I'm not asking you to trust an institution. I just showed you what institutions do with your trust. I'm asking you to look at what I did alone in that valley, and decide for yourself whether I'm someone worth building something with.
  > The door's open. Come find out.

  **Owner-facing, carried to `OBJ-67`:** two statements in it sit against the record and will be public once it airs — *'I'm System Rank S'* uses a term the world has no referent for (the System is on the Bearer-Only Register, Profile Section 14.4.1; his public standing is carded S-Rank), and *'a platform I wasn't officially standing on'* is contradicted by `OBJ-58`'s official access log, which names him and Sorcha Bellweather. Both are the character's own words, recorded as said, not corrected.

  Sanne: technically clean, one take, no redo — and, off-note, that she doesn't usually feel anything watching a monitor. Asked about the second half, she called it recommended, not needed. Alexander floated clearing an S-Rank Gate on camera; she named the real risk to two people with families and asked him to guarantee their safety. **He did — under one condition: how he does it and how he secures it can never leak.** She agreed at once and drafted a separate 'method' clause beyond the standard NDA: nobody who sees the method, herself included, can leak it and survive it financially; raw files never leave a drive she controls; the editor works only from a cut she has already made around it. His brief to the crew, without mechanism: a door where there wasn't one; a change they will feel and can't explain, which is what keeps them safe; nobody reacts to it or points a camera at him while it happens.

  Out of character between these beats: `/system skills` rendered (no state change; the panel mis-sourced Orenne's four ratified skills under 'Succession, the Assay champions' — corrected in the same session). A proposed Bulwark + Last Span stack was checked against the rules — reduction composes multiplicatively, **≈98.5%, never 100%**; Unwatched is self-only; Silent Step does share (9 beneficiaries)."
```

description: "**System shop: Instant-Dungeon Key [S-Rank], 900,000 g** (Profile Section 12.5). Gold 349,823,281 -> 348,923,281. Opened in Sanne's own studio at the player's direction — Section 17 requires no venue, and the key is unregulated space either way.

  **Protection, cast before the door:** The Last Span (22 Mana — Alexander plus the three of them, 85%, five exchanges), Bulwark ×2 (10 each, one per tech, `Braced elsewhere`), Silent Step (3, all three sharing the stealth step, `Unremembered` live). A door stood in the middle of the studio; the crew did not react, and Sanne's face did for exactly one second.

  **The instance (all real rolls):** archetype d8 **8 → Frozen Gallery** (Mana recovery halved inside, Section 5.2). Population at the key's S-Rank: **3d6 → 8 commons, 1d2 → 1 elite, 1 boss.** Alexander entered alone, Keen Sense (1 Mana) shared across the crew once they were in range, Flux Sight's automatic read: nothing hostile in the entry chamber. He waved them through; Sanne framed a shot before saying a word.

  **Daggers and Twin Fang only**, deliberately, so nothing on camera reads as more than a very good knife-fighter. Net +1 step. Eight commons, one strike each — naturals 74 (Vanguard opener), 99, 100, 13, 93, 47, 49, 54; effective 94, 100, 100, 33, 100, 67, 69, 74; all dead, DMG 5,507 standard against ~4,000 Rank Health. The elite: one Twin Fang combo, natural 38 → 58, dead before the off-hand finished. The boss (~16,000 Health): natural 71 → 91 on the opener (Godslayer +10% vs. a boss), then Twin Fang, natural 37 → 57 — two exchanges. **No damage taken.** Twin Fang's 5 Mana was charged twice at settlement; play had recorded Keen Sense as the only cost.

  **XP — owed by the rules and never totalled in play; settled here.** Kill XP at S-Rank, no underdog bonus (creature Rank = System Rank): 8 × 1,000 + elite 2,000 + boss 4,000 = 14,000; S-Rank Gate-clear milestone 3,200 (Section 17: a real clear). **+17,200: 7,642/19,700 → 24,842 → LEVEL 197 → 198, carry 5,142/19,800.** The level-up settled on the boss's death under Section 3.2: past level 100 the rung is +1 to every base Stat (2919/2644/2936/2645/3164 → **2920/2645/2937/2646/3165**), +5 unspent points (0 → **5**), maxima re-derived (**Health 11,796, Mana 6,372**), both restored to full. Not a decade level; no Title Assessment.

  **Loot:** 8 beast-drop S-Rank crystals; 2 S-Rank cores (elite, boss); boss drop d100 **46 → Rune** (41-65), d8 **5 → Flash Step [S-Rank]** — already known at S-Rank, so a duplicate, **banked intact** on the `EVT-001001` precedent. **Mineable deposit: 3d6 12 × 32 (S-Rank) + 2 (Prospector) = 386 crystals**, worked by hand while the crew filmed. 394 crystals and both cores stacked into one pile for the low-angle shot. Sanne: 'This shot alone is worth more than the fee.' **Same-day delivery**, as paid for — she edits it herself tonight (a four-to-five-day delivery line was player-caught and re-narrated; the whole premise of the premium was same-day).

  Led the crew out; stepped out last. **System shop: 394 S-Rank crystals @ 15,300 g = +6,028,200 g. Gold 348,923,281 → 354,951,481.** Cores banked, unsold (the shop refuses cores). Key spent; instance gone. **€80,000 wired to Sanne; booked at $80,000.00** — the figure play narrated (no EUR:USD rate has ever been fixed for this campaign; recorded without inventing one). Cash $540,258,490.22 → **$540,178,490.22.** Sanne, at the door: nobody hears any of it from her, ever — 'the paper's just what makes it enforceable.' And, genuinely: good luck with the guild.

  **Skill settlement (Section 7.4 promotion reconciliation).** Uses: Bulwark +2 (two activations), Silent Step +1, Keen Sense +1, Flux Sight +1 (the entry read the crew's safety turned on), Twin Fang +2, Dagger Mastery +11 (the chassis bonus applied on each resolved strike action: eight commons, the elite, two boss exchanges). The Last Span +1 use (no tracked counter; rendered on its line). Pre-empt not counted — no exchange's outcome turned on initiative, the `EVT-001001` reading. **No qualifying scene for any skill: a cleared instant dungeon at his own tier, every creature deleted before it could act, is uncontested by Section 7.4's own named example** — the `EVT-001001` precedent, and the player's own call on the next morning's `quiet_days` question. This clear therefore does not reset `quiet_days`."
```

description: "Anchored rift-step to the Denver facility (5 Mana). **The far door, 2,500 Mana** (Section 18.13.1), reopened in the containment room. Across to the Landing; Mapwork carried him straight to Tolo's archive (Mapwork +1 use).

  **The stolen archive, second read.** Tolo, already at the shelf holding Earth's file, asked for a second read before committing a finding — **Earth's check-interval has shortened again**, into the range where his other harvested worlds began accelerating toward their endpoints rather than idling near one (consistent with `EVT-000995`'s finding that the file tracks its own shortening interval). Tolo wrote it into his permanent record, not a working note — his tell that he has stopped treating it as provisional. **Quarantined: a narrated 'roughly 96% now, against 94%' reading.** It contradicts `110_WORLD_LEDGER.md`'s owner-ruled `grading_state` — the figure never advances on elapsed time, advances only on six named milestones (none occurred this span), and a stolen copy is not an instrument that reads it again. `read_pct` stays **94**; the interval finding stands.

  **The fifth teaching circuit — structured practice, compressed at the player's direction** ('all the skills he's teaching me and new ones, like always'; 'train with others too'; 'Orenne was teaching me too'). **Classified at this promotion under the player's own ruling (option B): every taught candidate on the record was worked this circuit, not only those the live narration named** — live play had advanced only the held skills and The Count/Tally, and told the player 'nothing crosses the threshold' against a stored state holding ten further taught candidates at 2/3. No dice were rolled in the circuit.

  - **Tolo** (`tolo-teaching-2026-09-20`): The Robbed Library, Cross-Reference, Four Seams Away 2/3 → **3/3, ratify**; The Long Read 1/3 → **2/3**; held skills Diagram Sense, Appraise, Trace each +1 qualifying scene (1/3 → 2/3). Two new candidates opened, 1/3: *The Marginal Note* and *What the Shelf Remembers* — **neither is on Tolo's authored list** (`worlds/gatefall/270_THE_FAR_SIDE.md` Section 4.2); owner authoring owed before either can ratify. Tolo: 'Neither's useful to me. Both are useful to you. That's usually how it goes.'
  - **Ilith** (`ilith-teaching-2026-09-20`): Not There, Away 2/3 → **3/3, ratify**; held Read the Want and Second Skin +1 scene (1/3 → 2/3), Unwatched +1 (0/3 → 1/3). New candidate *The Convenient Truth*, 1/3 — **unauthored on Ilith**, owner authoring owed.
  - **Sevrin** (`sevrin-teaching-2026-09-20`): Held Breath, Four Thousand Years' Patience 2/3 → **3/3, ratify**; held Closing Word +1 scene (1/3 → 2/3); Every Seam He Has Walked, Mapwork, Grip on the Rail worked, nothing to test against, no credit.
  - **Aumry** (`aumry-teaching-2026-09-20`): Call the Turn, Handover, Where the Weight Goes 2/3 → **3/3, ratify**; held Read the Learner +1 scene (1/3 → 2/3). New candidate *Second Set of Hands* (an ally briefly mirrors one of his techniques for a single use), 1/3 — **unauthored on Aumry**; nearest authored entry is The Ninth Lesson; owner ruling owed.
  - **Orenne** (`orenne-teaching-2026-09-20`): The Count, Tally 2/3 → **3/3, ratify.** Orenne, watching the second one settle: 'Good. Neither of those needed me to be wrong about the rest of it.'

  **Twelve ratifications, all pre-authored on the teacher and therefore automatic** (Profile Section 7.1, taught route: *the teacher's own authored entry supplies the result*), each entering at **Novice**, at `min(System Rank + 1 → S, the teacher's authored Rank)`, at the authored cost, `transmissible: true`. **The Count carries its authored 'Uncapped'**; its interaction with the engine's net ±3 modifier cap (Section 4.3) is surfaced to the owner, not resolved here.

  **~4 hours on the far side, 17:41-21:41.** Section 5.2.1's drain: Mana 3,872 → **1,323/6,372** (−2,549; no recovery accrues there). `far_side_notice` 79 → **90** (+3 door onto the Landing, +4 presence, +4 door standing open); thresholds 84 and 90 crossed, satisfied by the circuit's own contact with all five Standing — the `EVT-000941` precedent. Mapwork +1 more on the walk back to the Landing-side terminus."
```

description: "Back through to Denver, nothing standing in the doorway. The far door closes at the same flat 2,500 it opened for; with 1,323 in the pool, **a Greater Mana Potion was drunk (40 → 39) and the door closed** — Mana full, then 6,372 → 3,872. Established precedent (`EVT-001008`, `EVT-001011`); play chose the potion over the ~30-minute wait Conduit's continuous recovery would have needed, and the player did not object. Door currently **closed**; `gate_walker` opened/closed five times.

  The phone, back in signal: **one encrypted message from Sanne, delivered well before he returned.** Opened: the sit-down plays exactly as spoken, no seam where nerves would show; then the silent half — the Frozen Gallery's corridors, daggers only, the eight commons, the elite, the boss in two exchanges, the pile of nearly four hundred crystals and two cores in one unbroken shot, the voiceover re-cut tighter underneath. Watched cold, it reads as intended: a man telling you something true, then showing you he isn't exaggerating. **The finished cut sits encrypted with Alexander; nothing has gone anywhere yet.** Raw footage remains on Sanne's controlled drive under the NDA and the method clause."
```

## EVT-001039 - Kölsch, and the Real Account

```yaml
id: EVT-001039
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"

  - ENT-000124

    result: record-updated
    record: REL-000117
description: "Called Reinhardt — 'Now works. I'm home.' — and, on the thread he had texted about, he asked to actually have the Jiu Valley conversation this time rather than defer it again. Anchored rift-step to Brandhof's courtyard (anchor 40); he sent a pin fifteen minutes' walk away; **Sprint** made it under a minute. **Clock reconciled at promotion:** four hours on the far side put Frankfurt at roughly 05:00 on 2026-09-21, not evening — the narrated 'evening light' is quarantined; Reinhardt was up, home, and unhurried.

  A precise, uncluttered apartment. Alexander, laughing, admitted it was his second time in Germany and he still hadn't had German beer; Reinhardt produced two bottles of **Kölsch** ('don't let anyone from Munich hear you drinking it'). Then: 'So. Jiu Valley.'

  **'It was me who ended it there.'** Reinhardt: 'I know. I told you I knew' — and thanked him for the difference between concluding a thing and being told it. Asked for the real account, engineer to whatever Alexander is. **Alexander told him honestly:** Sorcha Bellweather walking him to the observation platform; her own second-year rotation and the three trainees she lost on a rescue she had been pulled from days before; the twenty-eighth anchor, set uncontested; the approach on foot under three stacked concealment methods; the read at roughly 45,000 Health and 7,200 Mana; three Rupture strikes; the first confirmed kill on something that had outlasted six S-Ranks and thirty-one thousand deaths across six years. Reinhardt: 'Three strikes' — and that Sorcha walking him to it herself is not a detail people get right guessing. He believes him.

  **Asked whether Sorcha knows he's been told: no.** Alexander said Reinhardt is now the only one who knows Sorcha's part, that it may not matter much longer — **he intends to go public, so it all lands on him and nobody else** — and that he wanted a board meeting now. Reinhardt's cost, stated plainly: the video can leave her name out and she is still three phone calls away for anyone who wants to find the BGM containment official with Jiu Valley rotation history who walked an unaffiliated S-Rank onto that platform. **Does she know she is carrying that risk?** (Reinhardt's first reply had him organising the board call himself — player-caught as overstepping; re-narrated: it should be Alexander's ask. His time-zone aside is quarantined as wrong — Prague and Frankfurt share an offset.) Handed the phone with the final cut, he watched it through without a word: 'That's not a recruitment video. That's a declaration with a recruitment pitch attached to it.' It's good; whoever cut it knows what she's doing. 'Call the others.'"
```

## EVT-001040 - The First Board Meeting: Five For, None Against

```yaml

description: "Alexander called all four others himself; Kane picked up fast, Cade after a moment, Tereza already sure something was up ('this is either very good or very bad'), Aubrac last, cutting short a morning interview. **Nine anchored hops, 45 Mana**: Reinhardt from his own kitchen to Winnetka, then Prague and back with Tereza, Paris and back with Aubrac, Kane's apartment and back with Kane, Denver and back with Cade — an ally he touches is sent with him (Section 18.8, S rung). Reinhardt, dry: 'Efficient.'

  **The Pendragon Guild's first board meeting, five members in one living room.** Alexander: 'I want to come clear to you and present you my pitch for the guild, and I want you to vote if I should go public with it.' The cut played on the television. **Aubrac and Cade learned for the first time, live, that Alexander ended the Jiu Valley Exclusion**; Kane and Tereza watched it as confirmation; Reinhardt had seen it an hour before.

  - **Aubrac:** professional instinct first — devastatingly well shot — then, the camera register gone: he went in alone, after six S-Ranks died trying. She looks at him differently now.
  - **Cade:** tests it for whether the anger is real. Thirty-one thousand people, six years, nobody official saying a word — **it should be public**; somebody should have to answer for the silence.
  - **Kane:** no taking it back, no controlled leak, no 'sources say' once it's out — **still thinks he should do it.**
  - **Tereza:** she watched a country decide what she was allowed to say about herself for three years; he gets to decide this once, before anyone does it for him. **Yes.**
  - **Reinhardt:** his reservation stands — Sorcha's exposure does not disappear because the room votes yes; it's a cost Alexander is choosing to carry. **Yes** — silence was only ever protecting the people who benefited from it.

  **Vote: five for, none against. Reinhardt's yes carries its stated caveat; it is not a dissent.**

  Alexander asked for help getting it on national television, other countries too, at whatever it costs, **and committed to the room that he will talk to Sorcha before it airs.** Offers: **Tereza** — Czech Television, national, guaranteed; she'll call in the morning; anything wider needs calls. **Aubrac** — France 2, TF1 with patience, or a streaming exclusive for speed; favours owed twice over; wants to see the final edit again before she puts her name behind placing it. **Kane** — no network time, but he can make sure BGM doesn't try to kill the story once it's out. **Cade** — no media contacts; she thinks it should happen. **Reinhardt** — Germany in a day or two; people adjacent to it, not people who own it; an answer by tomorrow. **Alexander: 'So we're airing in two days'** — targeting 2026-09-22 — he will work the US side himself and talk to Sorcha. Thanked them for assembling this fast and for honest votes. Aubrac, warmer than the room had seen her: he didn't have to ask them to vote, and that he asked anyway is why she said yes to the board. Tracked as `OBJ-67`."
```

description: "**Returns, reconciled at promotion to five anchored steps (25 Mana)**: Winnetka → Prague (Tereza, already on her phone) → Paris (Aubrac, a last warm look) → Frankfurt (Reinhardt, straight to his desk) → Denver (Cade, a brief nod) → home. Play narrated 'four returns, 20 Mana' with the guests vanishing while Alexander stayed put; the S rung sends an ally *with* him, so the leg home is owed and counted.

  **Kane stayed behind, not moving toward the door** ('that doesn't sound like Kane' — an over-analytical first line, player-caught and re-narrated to his own register): *'I could go home and lie to myself about being tired.'* **Alexander: 'Stay. I like you around.'** Kane, without a clever answer for once: 'Yeah. Ok.' — onto the couch, the front finally off. 'Long day. Good one, though.' Two beers; Kane's toast, *'to the last quiet night before you become impossible to ignore'*; a spell-checked word swapped in Alexander's reply was re-narrated at the player's request, Kane letting him have it: 'Debatable, but I'll let you have it tonight.' Alexander: to change something you have to sacrifice something. Kane noticed it was the most honest thing he'd said all night — and that the sacrifice he means isn't the anonymity.

  **Alexander sat beside him and leaned his whole side into him, beer in hand, tired; put a hand on his knee; then lay down with his head in Kane's lap, looking up at him, and thanked him.** Kane took the weight without making it more than it was, a hand at the back of his neck, then in his hair: 'Don't thank me. I didn't do anything except stay.' Alexander: glad he's here tonight — he wants to enjoy this while it's still kind of easy. Kane: then do that instead of talking about it — no board, no broadcast, no Sorcha, until one of them falls asleep. 'Deal.' **Fingers laced; Kane fell asleep first, still holding on.** Alexander moved them both to his bed with an in-scene rift-step (1 Mana) and fell asleep beside him, hands still held. **No sex this night** — the first time the two have shared a night that was only this.

  **Character firsts, observed (Decision 076):** Alexander asked Kane to stay for his company, in words ('I like you around'), rather than on logistics; Alexander's first unguarded physical rest on him (head in his lap); Kane's rare unmanaged smile and his voice dropping the dry register entirely."
```

## EVT-001045 - A Newsletter at Dawn, and Tuesday Becomes Today

```yaml
id: EVT-001045
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-21T06:00:00-05:00"

participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000242
  - ENT-000114
counter_deltas: []

  - subject: ENT-000242
    result: record-updated
    record: REL-000107
  - subject: ENT-000114
    result: record-updated
    record: REL-000106
description: "Woke at the 06:00 window. Kane was already up with two coffees, and he turned his phone around: **a newsletter piece by Galen Mercer**, a risk analyst writing for the reinsurance trade, posted 04:50 Chicago and already forwarded across Region V's hunter chats, titled *Who Was on the Platform?* It names nobody. It recaps last week's on-camera ask by a European Gate Council member and a House committee staffer for a working theory; notes that the Jiu observation platform runs on a cleared visitor list; reports that the Council's Actuarial Secretariat has filed a reciprocity request for that morning's access file; and closes on a BGM National doctrine official, whose office signs off on platform clearances, who 'did not respond to a request for comment.' (Standing reaction `jiu_valley_public_reaction`, stages 8 and 10, surfacing.) Kane read it as a guess: 'I'd bet that's your Sorcha. Somebody got to her door before you did.'

  Alexander: 'Means I need to move today.' He asked what TV channel and YouTuber would take his money and air it today. Kane: nobody takes the money. The networks won't clear a paid program same-day, but their news desks will fight over the exclusive. **Cass Orlov's *Past the Cordon*** (61 million subscribers, raid footage, same-day drops) would take the money or do it for free. Kane dislikes it; half the bad calls he has seen on a cordon started with someone hoping to end up there. Kane: the board voted to air it, not for Tuesday specifically, and today is better because the Bureau can't bury what's already out. And, flatly: 'You said Sorcha first, though.'

  **Tereza called at 06:07** (her morning commitment, met): **Czech Television confirmed**, *Události* at 19:00 Prague leading with it, and the full cut on ČT24 after it, no edits, in writing. The news director had read the Mercer piece and asked twice whether he would go early. **Alexander: today, 'I would rather have it only in Europe than be too late.'** Tereza: 'Your moment, not theirs.' She asked him to send the cut to her alone so she could walk it into the building herself, and said she would tell Lukas and Camille. **The cut was sent to Tereza at 06:13, one file to one recipient.** Kane took his phone out to the balcony."
```

description: "**Alexander called Sorcha Bellweather before it aired, as he had promised the board.** She picked up on the first ring; she had written 'call him at eight' down so she would stop picking the phone up. Galen Mercer had emailed her at 23:40 the night before, polite and specific, asking her to confirm who was on the list on the tenth. She had not answered. Incident Review had interviewed her for forty minutes on Thursday, and she told them what the log says and nothing past it. 'Is this coming out?'

  **Alexander: he is going public on his own terms, will take every eye on him, and if asked about her will say she knew nothing and that he tricked her into the clearance.** Sorcha refused the cover, precisely. The clearance was expedited on the record because he declared himself self-funded; the log shows her meeting him at Sector 4 and walking him up; 'she didn't know anything' turns her into an official who is either lying or incompetent. **Her own decision:** she kept the kill off her chain for eleven days because she didn't know what to write, and she is filing it this morning in person with her director: what she witnessed, when, and why it took this long, so the Bureau has it from her before his face is on a screen. 'Some of them are mine to carry.' **Airtime given: 1 PM Washington.** She will be in her director's office at twelve. 'Thank you for calling me first.'"
```

description: "Kane came in from the balcony ('Bureau's up early for a Monday') and, asked how to broadcast in the US, laid out three ways: a network desk (the biggest reach, but one Washington can call at 9:15 and ask to hold), Orlov (no one can call him), or his own account at the same minute Prague goes live (no one can ask it to hold). Whichever he picked, the desks would run it within five minutes of Czech TV anyway. 'If it were me, I'd want the first copy somewhere nobody I work for can reach.' **Alexander: 'So I will do it on my own on the internet.'** Kane: 'I'll keep Washington busy till then. That's the job I've got.' *The Ledger* pushed a follow-up at 06:25: the Council confirms the reciprocity request.

  **Camille Aubrac, called at Tereza's relay (06:30):** she had watched the cut again as its defender and would put her name behind it. Her one note was not about the cut: someone will hold the platform list up next to his face tonight, so the answer should already be his. **France 2 will run the full statement in the 20h news, one hour after Prague**, or a same-minute streaming exclusive. **Alexander: the 8 o'clock.** She confirmed by text at 06:51 ('they're thrilled and terrified').

  He let a BGM National Office of Incident Review call ring out (06:34). Its voicemail asked to speak before close of business. **He created the accounts:** a video channel and an X account, both named 'Alexander Pendragon', since `@AlexanderPendragon` was held by an unofficial fan account with 38,000 followers. The handle was **`@PendragonGuild`** (the player's choice), free on both. The long upload needed phone verification. **A premiere was scheduled for 12:00 Central**, same minute as Prague, titled with his name and nothing else. Tereza sent a photo from the Czech Television lobby: 'inside.'

  **Lukas Reinhardt called at 14:02 Frankfurt (07:02):** ZDF will lead *heute* at 19:00, the same minute as Prague, with the full statement streamed after. ZDF asked for someone they know to confirm the man is who he says, and Reinhardt will, by phone, off camera, only that: 'It costs me nothing I wasn't prepared to spend when I voted.' Then his actual question: had he spoken to her? **Alexander: minutes ago.** Reinhardt: then the risk is known to the one person it belongs to. He offered Brandhof's Washington lawyers, who answer to nobody there, if Sorcha ever needs counsel that isn't her own agency's, to be passed on by Alexander only, since she doesn't know Reinhardt knows. Kane, 07:06: 'Eggs are on.'"
```

At 1.4 km: police tape, a Winnetka cruiser (Officer **Dunleavy**, who had taken the flux-alarm call at 06:05 and taped it by 06:20), and a Region V survey tech guarding **a Gate on the sand, carded 'ASSESSED E-RANK (UNCONFIRMED)'**. **Flux Sight read it on sight: [S-Rank], confirmed by capability** (owner ruling C at `EVT-001052`: Flux Sight reads a Gate's true Rank from outside). The tech recognised both carded S-Ranks of Chicago. **'Stop. Is anyone inside?'** 🎲 63 (01-08 a person had gone in, 09-15 a dog, 16-00 nobody) → **nobody**; the tech and Dunleavy had walked the sand. Kane moved the tape back to the road and got the neighbouring houses checked.

  **Alexander called Renata at 07:43**: a Gate carded E that is an S. She would not reclassify on the record from a reading only he can take, but under the standing agreement she routes it on his word. **Section 9.11: 🎲 63 − 20 (standing agreement) = 43 → OFFERED.** A Region V survey drone was on its way. **Alexander: he can take it, and he had called so it would not go on the board as an E and get a crew sent in.** Renata pulled it from the Region V queue (listed as an E lot at 06:20, no bids yet) and put a National **hold** on it: no posting, no contract, no one past the tape, the survey crew included. The Gate is his, and he is not to go in before the drone reads it unless something comes out. 'Nobody is going in.' Kane held the sand.

  **He finished the run** (north loop to the Kenilworth line, 10/10 km) and, **back at the tape, did the rest of the 57th daily in front of the Gate**, which is established routine, so no roll: **complete 08:24, streak 44 → 45**, rewards pending, unclaimed. The deck neighbour's video (posted 07:58, reposted by the fan account) was past 9,000 views; three more neighbours stood at the road tape. **08:27, the drone read the interior: confirmed S-Rank, Shattered City** (the player's settlement: an ordinary S-Rank Gate, whose 06:00 remote reading was simply wrong). Renata, 08:31: 'Your read was right, and it's on paper now,' with a two-day timer from 06:00, the hold standing, and the Gate his. Kane: 'Your eyes and the drone agree.' The drone withdrew to Region V's tech at the tape. **Correction, `EVT-001053` (2026-09-21, player-caught on resuming this checkpoint):** an earlier draft of this record additionally stated that Alexander asked for the drone and took it in on follow mode. He did not; that sentence was a Runtime authoring error with no player declaration behind it (`F-075`). Struck. The drone's involvement in this Event ends here, at the tape, with a Rank confirmation and nothing else."

description: "**Entry: true Rank S** (read from outside). Anomaly rolled twice with the more dangerous kept (declared order: confluence > red gate > population > twist): 🎲 42 and 🎲 7, both *population one Rank above*, which at S becomes **doubled population at S**. 3d6 🎲 3+3+3 = 9 → **18 commons (Scaffold Hounds)**, 1d2 🎲 2 → **4 elites (Gantry Brutes)**, and **1 boss (the Keystone)**. Archetype Shattered City, drone-confirmed. Flux Sight read the room: yellow, commons 4000/2000, elites 8000/2000, boss 16000/2000.

  **Resolution, all real rolls.** Net +3 on every one of his attacks: +2 Agility margin (2,701 vs S domain 60), +1 in-scene Rift-step before each attack, capped. Effective = natural + 60. A natural 01-05 is a fumble, and effective 96+ reads as the top band ×4 (the `EVT-001036` precedent). **Pre-empt** resolves him first every exchange. **Not There** makes the first attack against him each exchange miss. Monsters attack at −3 (his Agility margin and Broken Rhythm), so only a natural 96-100 could land. **Reduction 90.25%** (armor 72.93%, Loner 10%, Warlord's Loop 60%). **The Count** is +10% per exchange already fought (`EVT-001043`).
  - **Ex 1:** Rift-step + Dimensional Projection ×6 (4 Longswords [S], Quickknife [S] Red Gate, Ghost Quickknife [A]), Vanguard +30%. 89→100, 57→100, 46→100, **03 fumble**, 08→68, 65→100: **5 hounds**. Weakest landed hit: (2,956+22)×1.95 = 5,807 ×1.3 = 7,549 vs 4,000. Attacks: first auto-miss, 72, 01, 90, all miss.
  - **Ex 2:** Projection ×6 from banked spares (Greatarm [A] ×2, Quickknife [A], Quickknife [B], Ghost Quickknife [B], Longsword [B]), Count +10%. 24→84, **05 fumble**, 90→100, **05 fumble**, 74→100, 74→100: **4 hounds**. Attacks: the first, **a natural 100**, auto-missed by Not There; 29, 36, 78, 93, 05, 65, 35, 41, all miss.
  - **Ex 3:** Twin Fang, Count +20%; all 12 thrown weapons recalled as the exchange's one inventory change. 32→92 (5,509 ×1.2 ×2 = 13,222); off-hand 76 at +2 → 100 (×3.35 ×4): **2 hounds**. The Brutes arrived and the Keystone threw masonry (a minor ability, MP −200): first auto-miss, then 71, 03, 55, 58, 08, 34, 66, 59, 36, 53, boss 60, all miss.
  - **Ex 4:** Projection ×6, recalled weapons, Count +30%. 21→81, 33→93, 52→100, 61→100, 55→100, 64→100: **6 hounds → LEVEL 199.** Attacks: first auto-miss, 07, 68, 48, 45, boss 40, all miss.
  - **Ex 5:** Rupture on Brute 1, weapons recalled, Count +40%. 67→100: (1,000+3,187)×3.05 = 12,770 ×1.4 ×4 = 71,512: **Brute 1**. Attacks: first auto-miss, 13, 58, 86, boss 54, all miss.
  - **Ex 6:** Projection ×6 (hound ×1; Brutes 2 and 3 ×2 each; Brute 4 ×1), Count +50%. 55→100; B2 06→66 (5,809 ×1.5 = 8,714 ≥ 8,000) and 35→95; B3 33→93 and 43→100; B4 14→74 (Ghost Quickknife [A] 5,500 ×1.5 = **8,250**, 250 over): **last hound and Brutes 2-4.** The Keystone's one attack auto-missed.
  - **Ex 7:** Rupture on the Keystone; Godslayer ×1.10, Giantslayer ×1.15, Count +60%. 63→100: 12,770 ×1.10 ×1.15 ×1.60 ×4 = **103,382** vs 16,000, dead before it acted. **Gate cleared → LEVEL 200.** Two-hour clear window, collapse ~10:37.

  **No damage taken. Mana:** 6,372 → −5 −5 −6 −5 = 6,351 → Level 199 restores **6,374** → −11 −5 −11 = 6,347 → Level 200 restores **6,376/6,376**. **Health** untouched, maxima 11,796 → 11,800 → **11,804/11,804**.

  **XP (Sections 3.3-3.4, S content at System Rank S, no underdog):** 18 × 1,000 + 4 × 2,000 + boss 4,000 × 2 (**Godslayer: a boss kill grants double Kill XP**) + S-Rank clear milestone 3,200 = **+37,200**. 5,142/19,800 → 22,142 at ex 4 → **Level 199**, carry 2,342/19,900 → 22,542 at ex 7 → **Level 200**, carry **2,642/20,000**. Two level-ups: +1 to every base Stat each (past level 100 the rung is +1), **+5 unspent each (5 → 15)**, maxima re-derived, pools restored.

  **Title Assessments, settled inside the Level-200 exchange (Section 16.1):** **Level 190, retroactive**: `EVT-001001` crossed 186 → 197 and the 190 assessment never fired. Read against the record as it stood then, **none granted**. **Level 200: none granted.** Deathless, Worldmark and The Standing Name stay flagged, not ruled; **The Called** joins them (7-8 accepted-and-cleared Section 9.11 assignments on a clear reading, with Argyle Street and Newark ambiguous). Executioner and The Ledger's End stay foreclosed. Titles held 20/33, slots 8/8, next assessment 210.

  **Qualifying scene: none.** S-Rank content against Combat Tier S that could not have made him fail at a cost is uncontested (the `EVT-000944`/`EVT-001036` precedent), so skill uses are credited and no scenes are. `quiet_days` is not reset. Uses credited where the outcome turned: Not There (the ex 2 natural 100) and The Count (Brute 4's kill needed it). Pre-empt and Broken Rhythm are not counted, since no outcome turned on either (the `EVT-001036` reading)."
```

description: "**Loot (Sections 11.1-11.2):** beast drops, one crystal per beast killed, **23 S-Rank crystals** (18 + 4 + 1; the rule counts every beast, where two earlier records had counted only commons); **5 S-Rank cores** (4 elites, the boss); **boss drop 🎲 d100 61 → Rune, d8 4 → Stone Skin [S-Rank]**, a duplicate, banked intact, giftable (public-Gate boss instruction). **Deposit 3d6 🎲 2+6+1 = 9 × 32 + 2 (Prospector) = 290 crystals**, worked out by hand 08:37-09:02, the drone recording. **All 12 banked spares recalled.** Total **313 S-Rank crystals, 5 S-Rank cores**, all earmarked for the federal lot.

  **Out at 09:03.** The tech pulled the drone's card, which held ninety seconds of Rank-confirmation reading — nothing else; **the follow-mode video attributed to this Event by an earlier draft is struck as of `EVT-001053`** (`F-075`), and no recording of the clear itself exists. Kane: 'Not a scratch.' The seam stands until the window closes ~10:37; Dunleavy kept the tape up. **Filed to Renata by text, standing solo terms:** 'Out. Cleared, S-Rank, solo, no casualties. 313 S-Rank crystals and 5 S-Rank cores recovered. Selling for USD.' **Renata, 09:12:** filed and countersigned. **$500,000 flat + $6,000,000 severity**, her reading of the formula: an S-Rank Gate on a public beach carded E, a survey crew thirty minutes from walking in, contained before anyone was hurt, 'I'm not making you chase it.' Both processed by end of day. S-Rank material has no posted price and goes to the federal lot at the standing rate (~$400,000/crystal, ~$1,000,000/core), **~$130,200,000 estimated, lot closing Thursday 2026-09-24 17:00 ET**, transfer after close. She has asked Region V to route the ninety-second instrument card to her desk before anyone else reviews it. The deck video was past 40,000 views."
```

description: "**Home to wait, ~2h45m skipped at the player's instruction; what reached him arrived as it happened.** 10:20: a Chicago station's news van across from the gate and a reporter's stand-up. No one rang (standing reaction `pendragon_public_profile`, stage 24). The deck video passed 200,000 views; the fan account captioned it 'Pendragon clears a Gate before breakfast.' 10:55: the hunter chats found `@PendragonGuild`'s noon premiere, and the waiting count went from 0 to 11,000. **11:04, Sorcha:** 'Filed. In person, on the record, all of it.' Her director read it twice and thanked her for bringing it herself. **11:17: Incident Review's formal email** requests a recorded interview 'at your earliest availability this week', with no deadline. 11:31: the platform emailed that it had received a request concerning the premiere; 11:46: no action taken. **11:34, Reinhardt:** 'ZDF confirmed.' **11:40, Camille:** France 2, full statement, 'they moved the weather.' **11:50, Tereza:** a photo from the Czech Television control room, 'everyone here is pretending to be calm.'

  **Kane (11:47), unasked:** 'The platform thing. That was ours. Legal at National wanted it held for review. It's handled.' He did not say how. His secret and agenda (`worlds/gatefall/220_NOTABLE_FIGURES.md`) hold the cost, and nothing in this Event says what it was. Alexander, grinning: 'Let's shatter the world. Possibly mine.' Kane: 'It was already yours. You're just telling it so.'

  **12:00:00, it aired**, simultaneously on `@PendragonGuild` (14,286 waiting), ČT24 after *Události*'s lead, and ZDF *heute*, with France 2 due at 13:00. The statement as recorded: 'I'm an S-Rank hunter' (player-corrected at `EVT-001052`), the Jiu Valley account, the guild pitch, then the silent daggers-only clear. Kane's hand found his halfway through. **12:04:** the first US cable network cut into its noon hour with the ČT feed. **12:05:** the premiere passed 90,000 live; *The Ledger* alert: 'Chicago S-Rank Pendragon claims Jiu Valley kill in self-released statement.' **12:06:** the phone would not stop; **Renata Okonkwo-Vance calling**, unanswered at the cut."
```

description: "**Promotion barrier for `EVT-001043`-`EVT-001051`, at the player's `/save`.** Closing instant 2026-09-21 12:06 Chicago: the broadcast has just gone live and Renata is calling.

  **Audit matrix:**
  - **`EVT-001043`** (23:40, rulings) — no activation. Candidates settled; Backread mastery 0/3 → 2/3, scenes 3 → 5; streak 42 → 44; 54th box streak-upgraded.
  - **`EVT-001044`** (06:00 tick) — none. 23:40 → 06:00 asleep, resting, pools full, carries 0.
  - **`EVT-001045`**-**`EVT-001047`** (06:00-07:06) — no activation, no Mana.
  - **`EVT-001048`** (07:06-08:32) — Flux Sight +1 (the tape read). Run and daily, no roll; 57th daily complete, streak 45. Mode active for the run, pools full.
  - **`EVT-001049`** (08:33-08:37) — Rift-step +7, Dimensional Projection +4, Twin Fang +1, Dagger Mastery +5 (four volleys and one Twin Fang, each a strike action carrying the chassis bonus), Rupture +2, Flux Sight +1; Not There and The Count one use each (rendered, no tracked counter); Pre-empt and Broken Rhythm not counted. Uncontested, no qualifying scene. Mana 6,372 → 6,351 → 6,374 (Level 199) → 6,347 → **6,376** (Level 200). Health full throughout, max → **11,804**. XP +37,200.
  - **`EVT-001050`** (08:37-09:13) — no activation. Inventory +313 S crystals, +5 S cores, +1 Stone Skin rune [S]; 12 weapons recalled.
  - **`EVT-001051`** (09:13-12:06) — none. Resting at home from ~09:30, pools full, carries 0.

  **Reconciled totals.** **Level 198 → 200**, XP 5,142/19,800 → **2,642/20,000**. Base Stats 2920/2645/2937/2646/3165 → **2922/2647/2939/2648/3167**; effective **2958/2703/2951/2648/3188**. Unspent points 5 → **15**; `progression.ability_points_earned` 808 → **818**. Pending: Ability Points 8 → **20**, Status Recovery 37 → **40**, Daily Random Box 2 → **5** (#61 streak-upgraded). Health **11,804/11,804**, Mana **6,376/6,376**, both resting, carries 0. `skills.rift_step.successful_uses` 140 → **147**; Dimensional Projection 37 → **41**; Twin Fang 170 → **171**; Dagger Mastery 578 → **583**; Rupture 194 → **196**; Flux Sight 152 → **154**. Damage previews re-derived at eff. Strength 2,958 / Intelligence 3,188. Gold unchanged. Cash unchanged at $540,178,490.22; **pending payouts 0 → 2** ($6,500,000 due today 17:00 CT; ~$130,200,000 federal lot, closing 2026-09-24 17:00 ET). `quiet_days` **9**, not reset; the next tick reads 10. `world_clock.last_ticked` **2026-09-21T06:00**.

  **Owner rulings recorded this session, beyond `EVT-001043`:** **(C) Flux Sight reads a Gate's true Rank from outside**, including a Gate whose true Rank a rule resolves only on entry. While Deep Water is unequipped it therefore adds only the option to refuse at the threshold. **The Winnetka Gate was an ordinary S-Rank Gate**: the 06:00 remote reading was wrong, and any instrumented survey reads it true. This supersedes the Runtime's out-of-character reading that instruments would keep showing E. **The filmed statement reads 'I'm an S-Rank hunter' and never contained the platform sentence**: the protagonist's voluntary speech, player-authored; the `EVT-001035` quotation stands corrected by this ruling, not rewritten. Sprint's speed, The Count and ruling (C) all owe a profile amendment (Profile 1.118), not made at this checkpoint.

  **Flagged, not ruled:** **Godslayer's double boss Kill XP was not applied at `EVT-000944` (10 bosses), `EVT-001036` (1) and likely `EVT-001001` (10)**, about 84,000 XP never credited. Applied from here forward; the backfill is an open owner question. **The Called** needs an accepted-assignment count ruling. **New named figures not minted** (mentioned, not met): Galen Mercer (analyst), Cass Orlov (*Past the Cordon*), Officer Dunleavy (Winnetka PD). Dunleavy's name was spoken before its collision check, which came back clean at promotion.

  **Canon reconciliation:** everything above promoted. Corrections made live and re-narrated: the merge framing (item 2 of `EVT-001043`); the statement's text; the drone's reading (instruments read E → read S). **Minted:** none. New high-water mark: **EVT-001052**.

  Checkpoint follows."
```

description: "**12:06-12:33, the Winnetka house, the broadcast still live on three screens.** Renata Okonkwo-Vance called as the premiere aired. Three items, in the order she gave them: (1) the $500,000 flat + $6,000,000 severity from the lakefront clear releases by 17:00 today, and the federal lot (~$130,200,000) is unchanged, closing 2026-09-24; (2) the survey drone's card — **corrected in play (`F-075`): the player established he only ever asked the drone to confirm the Gate's true Rank, never to enter on follow mode.** The card holds ninety seconds of instrument reading and nothing else; no recording of the clear exists. Region V, the auction (routine provenance copy), and a **formal instrument-record request from BGM National's Office of Incident Review, filed 12:14 — six minutes after the broadcast** — all want it. **Signed by Deputy Director Imogen Strand**, not line reviewer Harlan Boyce (who has been calling since the 16th and left 06:34's voicemail unanswered); Strand's request also asks for the routing record behind Renata's own hold, which Renata named as her own exposure, not Alexander's. (3) An Associate Director for Field Operations' office called Renata at 12:11 asking how quickly Alexander could be in Washington; Alexander's answer, relayed: *send the agenda, might find time.* Kane, unprompted: *'Whatever they say the meeting's about — it isn't,'* and separately, of the request: *'That's not a reviewer.'*

  **12:48, the gate intercom.** Cass Orlov (`ENT-000284`, minted this Event — disposition authored before her first line per Decision 092), *Past the Cordon* (61M subscribers), arrived in person holding a long-lens photo of Alexander and Elias Kane together at the lakefront tape that morning. Pitched a 20-minute interview, offered to obscure Kane's identity if asked, and was candid about the trade she was making. **Alexander declined to commit and took her number instead** — no interview granted, no promise made either way. Kane's read, given after she left: he minded hearing his own name through the intercom from a stranger, said so plainly once, and did not press further.

  **13:00, France 2 aired the statement whole**, uncut, with a same-broadcast correspondent segment naming that **the European Gate Council will issue a formal statement at 09:00 Brussels time tomorrow** and declining to say whether it will name Alexander. Camille Aubrac texted at 13:07: *'It ran whole. They didn't cut a word... Sleep at some point.'*

  No roll this Event — no uncertain action resolved. Pools unchanged throughout: Health 11,804/11,804, Mana 6,376/6,376, both full and resting, carries 0."
```

## EVT-001054 - The Pendragon Guild Recruitment Framework, and the Ten-City Tour

```yaml
id: EVT-001054
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
kind: work

description: "**13:20-14:19, the Winnetka house.** The `@PendragonGuild` inbox, live since noon, read: 410,000 subscribers, 870,000 followers, 11,400+ message requests and climbing, most quoting the broadcast's guild pitch back — every Rank, no Gate run understrength, a direct line to Alexander. Seven of Kesha Morrison's own Coalition regulars were already in the replies. An Ironline Dispatch memo threatened exclusivity-clause forfeiture against any contracted hunter who applied elsewhere; **Alexander ruled it toothless** — Ironline has been suspended from Coalition bidding since BGM's formal case (`EVT-000836`) and has no live contracts to forfeit — and the trade's own replies read it the same way. Horizon posted a copied 'Crew Floor Guarantee' at 13:30, near-verbatim (`the_unnecessary_trade` surfacing).

  **Alexander called Kesha Morrison** to build a real application process. Settled, in order: automated card-registry verification replacing any human reading applications (unlicensed artificers held in a separate queue pending counsel); automated roster math (headcount and Rank-level checked against a Gate's requirement before a crew is cleared); a confirmation-reply plus an entry-point location ping (logged at the Gate's posted coordinates immediately before entry, not attempted from inside — Kesha's correction, accepted); a small random human audit layer on top, percentage undecided, catching what Wade Bishop's mathematically-valid-but-falsified Ironline rosters proved computers alone would not; and a **Pendragon Guild app** opened as a real follow-on project, entry-logging designed in from the start, a developer lead owed from Kesha by the next day. **Alexander named himself interim audit-escalation owner**, co-flagged to Kesha, until a real hire exists. Live tonight (18:00): intake form, registry check, roster math, confirm-reply, entry ping. Chicago's recruitment event secured for tomorrow at the Coalition's overflow hall, Kesha's own arrangement, no cost.

  **A ten-day, fifteen-city recruitment tour was set**, days 1-7 riding existing Riftwalker anchors (Chicago→Washington→Prague→Frankfurt→New York→Boston→Miami→Houston→Paris→Amsterdam→Dallas→Los Angeles→Seattle→San Francisco, two cities most days), days 8-10 by booked commercial flight from Amsterdam through cities with no standing anchor: London, Stockholm, Kyiv, Moscow, Istanbul, Tel Aviv. Kesha flagged, unresolved: no known local Gate-authority contact yet exists for Kyiv, Moscow, Istanbul, or Tel Aviv — she is working it in parallel, and Alexander may be the one finding the answer on the ground.

  No roll this Event — logistics and worldbuilding, no uncertain action. Pools unchanged: Health 11,804/11,804, Mana 6,376/6,376, full and resting throughout, carries 0. No fictional time gap requiring settlement — continuous conversation, ~14:19 close."
```

No Stat, skill counter, gold, or cash change. `quiet_days` **9**, unchanged — no day boundary crossed. `world_clock.last_ticked` **2026-09-21T06:00**, unchanged.

## EVT-001056 - The Lakefront Walk: Guild Tiers, the Venue, and the Texts

```yaml
id: EVT-001056
canonical_record: REC-000079

    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000130
    result: record-updated
    record: REL-000115
description: "**14:19-14:44, the Winnetka house then the lakefront.** Kane stayed behind at the house — a text buzzed his phone mid-broadcast-afterglow, deflected as 'nothing, later problem,' left unresolved this span. Alexander walked the private shoreline alone.

  **Guild tiering set by text (14:02-14:17).** Kesha's registry check came back clean and the roster math held, but she flagged the actual bottleneck: tier logic before 17:00 or the night's launch slips, and separately that 11,400+ applicants likely overflow the Coalition's own hall tomorrow. Alexander's guidance: tiers by a combination of confirmed Rank and verified field experience, top tier fast-tracked into the first intake window, lower tiers staged into later ones, nobody turned away outright — 'scale the schedule, not the door.' Kesha called it workable and flagged the venue question in the same breath; Alexander authorized booking the Wacker convention center at whatever it costs. She confirmed within minutes: locked inside the hour, public overflow release by end of day.

  **The world answered without being asked.** Walking the lakefront, five texts landed in quick succession from people who actually know him rather than the machinery running underneath him: Tanya Voss ('Saw it... Good'), Wade Bishop (congratulations, and a needling note about who was recruiting first), Diane Halloran (proud, an open offer to come by), Ada Reyes (a manifest/logistics note, offering to help scale it right), and — arriving a full minute later than the rest — Owen: 'Saw it. All of it. You looked — I don't know. Proud of you, for what it's worth. Take care of yourself, Alexander.' Alexander replied 'Thank you.' Owen, after visibly typing twice: 'Yeah. Take care of yourself.' Nothing further.

  No roll this Event — texting and a walk, no uncertain action. Pools unchanged: Health 11,804/11,804, Mana 6,376/6,376, full and resting throughout, carries 0."
```

description: "**14:44-14:52, the Denver containment facility.** Anchored step from the lakefront (flat 5 Mana; pool 6,376 -> 6,371, this Event's opening cost).

  **Called Sorcha Bellweather** to ask whether the facility could double as guild headquarters while still carrying the cage under construction. She advised against it on engineering grounds: the design's real value is partly that nobody knows where it is, and a public guild HQ on the same address puts hundreds or thousands of people onto that address regardless of interior access control. Proposed a badge-walled split property as a fallback; when Alexander pointed out the split still exposes the general location, she conceded the point immediately rather than defending the first answer, and recommended a fully separate building for the guild instead, with no shared vendor or filing trail back to Denver. Alexander agreed and stated intent to find one through realtors — not yet acted on.

  **Called Renata Okonkwo-Vance**, asking directly what she thought Deputy Director Strand's 'I already know what your answer's going to be' actually meant. Her read: Strand's office has stopped asking questions and started building a file on the assumption Renata is compromised by association, regardless of what she actually files — the same pattern she's watched end other people's careers, now aimed at her over him. Alexander told her he'd back whatever she decided and didn't care about his own exposure — the footage genuinely shows nothing. That removed his stake from her calculus: she decided to file both the drone card and a written account of her own reasoning for the National hold and drone dispatch, her name on the record rather than a bare data handoff, before Strand's own 17:00 deadline rather than at it. Read as genuinely moved by the backing.

  No roll this Event — two calls, no uncertain action. Pools: Mana 6,376 -> 6,371 (one anchored step). Health unchanged, 11,804/11,804."
```

## EVT-001058 - The Full Circuit: Five Standing, a Landing Finally Anchored

```yaml
