description: "**14:52-18:52, the far side.** Far door opened at the Denver facility (flat 2,500 Mana; pool 6,371 -> 3,871).

  **Tolo, the Ninth Quiet (anchor 36).** Anchored step, 5 Mana (3,871 -> 3,866). Asked for anything new from the stolen archive: a fresh comparison across his three other complete grading records found every one held a steady check-interval until its final stretch, where it began compressing — Earth's is compressing now, nowhere near that stretch by his own read. Pressed for a cause, he floated one guess, explicitly labeled as a guess: Alexander may be the variable, the first candidate to examine the exam rather than only survive it. Real practice on reading the Ninth Quiet's own diagrams alongside him (Diagram Sense crossed Novice -> Apprentice). Roughly ninety minutes total drain (3,866 -> 2,485 across two training passes, resolved as the promotion correction below records).

  **Aumry, the Long Shelf (anchor 35).** Anchored step, 5 Mana (2,485 -> 2,480). Extended training session, drain running to 993 across two passes; he named the toll on Alexander's face plainly and, without being asked twice, urged heading home once it showed — his own history with nine dead candidates behind the warning. Read the Learner crossed Novice -> Apprentice.

  **The Landing — anchor finally set.** Walked from Tolo's ground to the seam and on to the Landing (35 min, standard drain) rather than the invented return trip first narrated and corrected in play. Ilith was present, aware the door had stood open roughly two hours — the longest window in her four thousand years watching this exact ground — and asked plainly for it to stay open longer. Declined ('not today'). **Anchor 43 set** — the gap raised and left open at `EVT-001037`/`EVT-001038` finally closed. Crossed to Denver (free, door already open); Mana read 617, insufficient to close the door (2,500 needed). One **Greater Mana Potion** drunk at Denver — off-world rule does not apply on Earth, full restore, 617 -> 6,376; inventory 39 -> 38.

  **Ilith, back through the door.** Crossed again (free). Trained further: Read the Want crossed Novice -> Apprentice. ~50 min drain, 6,376 -> 5,845.

  **Sevrin, deep in the Stacks (anchor 32).** Anchored step, 5 Mana (5,845 -> 5,840). Quiet, deliberate session in his usual register; Closing Word crossed Novice -> Apprentice. Noted, without pushing, that his standing question (process-ending or a passed Earth) remains unanswered; accepted the deferral again. ~50 min drain, 5,840 -> 5,309.

  **Orenne, the Long Shelf.** Anchored step to Aumry's ground (5 Mana, 5,309 -> 5,304) plus a short walk across the valley (15 min, no fixed leg authored, treated as minor — 5,304 -> 5,145). Practiced Tally (+1 use). Noted this was the first time Alexander has trained with all five Standing in one sitting, without pressing for why. **Anchor 44 set** at his own ground, uncontested — his in-fiction reaction line was struck and re-narrated under the same-session anchor-perception ruling below.

  **Return.** Anchored step to the Landing, 5 Mana (4,667 -> 4,662, after the Orenne-leg training drain of ~45 min, 5,145 -> 4,667). Crossed to Denver (free). **Far door closed**, 2,500 Mana (4,662 -> 2,162).

  **Player ruling recorded this span (Closed Channels, `130_NPCS_AND_FACTIONS.md`):** none of the five Standing have any channel to Alexander's own act of setting a rift anchor, regardless of their own anchor-adjacent techniques (Sevrin's Anchor Denial, Ilith's Touch and Go, etc. — those read *existing* anchors and seams, not the private act of setting one). Applied retroactively within this span to the Landing and Orenne's-ground settings; no prior session's anchor-setting is affected.

  **Promotion correction (player-caught, this barrier).** In-session narration presented Tolo as teaching a still-open 'The Long Read' and several other legs as opening fresh 1/3 technique candidates. The live sheet going into this session already carried nearly the entire far-side curriculum as ratified (fifth circuit, `EVT-001037`), and 'The Long Read' specifically had already been merged into Backread at `EVT-001043`. **No new techniques were granted this session.** The five training legs are promoted instead as real practice reps against already-ratified, still-mastering entries: **Diagram Sense, Read the Learner, Read the Want, and Closing Word each crossed Novice -> Apprentice; Tally +1 use.** The fictional beats (visiting and training with all five Standing in one day) stand; the specific mechanical grants narrated in the moment do not.

  No roll this Event — training and travel, no uncertain or contested action; no combat. Pools: Mana 6,371 -> 2,162 net across the full circuit (see per-leg figures above). Health unchanged throughout, 11,804/11,804, no injury, no drain crossing into Health at any point (pool never reached 0)."
```

  Anchored step to the shelf (34th anchor). Activated Silent Step (Mana 3), Unwatched (Mana 5), and Unremarked Movement (free, passive) before moving. Swept the area with Keen Sense (Mana 1), Flux Sight (passive), and What Is Left (Mana 8, first credited success on this technique) — confirmed the shelf itself undisturbed but detected three presences several aisles off, resolved via Flux Sight as sheet-based System-bearers (no Rank at all — corrected in play after an initial, wrong 'B-Rank' read; nothing in the Assay's own domain carries a Rank, the same rule the far side runs on).

  Followed at a distance, stealth holding. Closed in and used Diagram Sense (Mana 12) on the notation one worker was tracing across the shelf-marks — not idle maintenance, an **independent interval-check reading on Earth's own file**, run from the Assay's own side, unconnected to anything Alexander or Tolo has done. Real external corroboration of Tolo's own interval-compression finding.

  **Capture: five Binding Grip Subdue rolls, all successful, no resistance capable of mattering.**
  - Target 1 (total surprise, unwitnessed): 🎲 58, net +3 (surprise +2, capability gap +1) -> effective 100, legendary. Clean, silent.
  - Target 2 (Unwatched dropped on the first attack; partial surprise lost): 🎲 54, net +1 (capability gap only) -> effective 74, strong success.
  - Target 3 (fully alerted): 🎲 57, net 0 (capability gap +1, active reaction -1) -> effective 57, standard success — one cut-off sound escaped.
  - Reactivated Unwatched (Mana 5). Woke all three at the Denver facility later this span (see `EVT-001061`); two were re-subdued once one (later identified as Anton Drury, `ENT-000285`, minted) revealed suspicion the space wasn't empty: 🎲 14, net +3 (already restrained, capability gap) -> effective 74, strong. 🎲 76, net +3 -> effective 100, legendary.

  Anchored step to Denver with all three captives via touch (5 Mana) — a genuine extension of the S-Rank passenger-transport grant beyond a single willing ally, granted in fiction as a provisional mechanic per Resident Core discipline, priced at the step's own ordinary cost, owed a real ruling against Section 18.8's ally-only wording.

  Mana this Event: door open (2,500) + anchored step to shelf (5) + Keen Sense/What Is Left (9) + Diagram Sense (12) + five Binding Grip rolls (100) + Unwatched reactivation (5) + anchored step to Denver (5) = **2,636 spent**. Health unchanged, 11,804/11,804, no damage taken or dealt beyond the non-lethal takedowns."
```

## EVT-001061 - Interrogation, Correction, and a Choice

```yaml
id: EVT-001061
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-resume
  game_date: "2026-09-21T19:10:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-16"
type: Event

  - ENT-000285
counter_deltas: []
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: record-updated
    record: ENT-000125
  - subject: ENT-000285
    result: record-updated
    record: ENT-000285
description: "**19:10-19:45, the Denver facility.** Woke all three captives without revealing presence (Unwatched held — waking is not an attack). They conferred in their own language, procedural rather than panicked, until the third (Drury) voiced genuine suspicion that something was listening.

  **A language-barrier premise was raised, played out across several exchanges (Second Skin + Diagram Sense combined on spoken structure, 🎲 87 net +1 -> effective 100 legendary comprehension breakthrough; a later free-composition attempt, 'Where is the authority?', 🎲 67 net +1 -> effective 87, strong success), and then corrected at the player's own prompt: no real language barrier has ever existed between Alexander and any far-side or Assay figure in this campaign — Tolo, Aumry, Ilith, Sevrin and Orenne have all spoken directly understandable dialogue throughout, and the Auditor's own record describes her being 'met, questioned' with no translation mechanic ever established.** Struck at this barrier: both rolls, and the Second Skin/Diagram Sense combined-comprehension provisional mechanic, are void. The scenes themselves (silence-pressure interrogation, vocabulary echo-prompts) are kept as narrated color; no new capability is promoted from them, and neither technique's Mana spend this span (Second Skin 8, Diagram Sense 12, both already counted in the Event totals) buys anything beyond its own ordinary listed effect.

  **Drury, once he understood he could simply be understood, gave a real, extended account** under sustained pressure (mostly silence, which repeatedly worked better than direct questioning): his own recruitment (a failed candidate, offered filing work over continuing alone in his world's ruins); the interval-check assignment (eight cycles on Earth's file specifically, unrequested); that the deep-read request on Earth's file originated from Reviewer rank or above, no name available to him; that **there is nothing between Reviewer and the Adjudicator** in anything his own rotation has ever touched, and that nobody at his level has seen an Adjudicator and lived to log it — external corroboration of the name Sevrin's own established secret already withheld. Drew a limited, honest map (his own personally-walked route only: staging section, this reading section, the interval-filing drop) on the facility's bare concrete floor; Alexander photographed it rather than attempting to store the floor itself.

  **Pressed on complicity, Drury answered honestly rather than deflecting** — confirmed it does bother him, the first time he's said so aloud to anyone, framed as a coping mechanism rather than indifference. Alexander stated a real position in response, unprompted and cold: he would rather cease to exist than help the Assay destroy another world. It visibly moved Drury.

  **Offered a choice: death, or far-side isolation** (informed honestly — the five Standing, who mostly avoid each other, no real community and no real threat). Drury asked for clarification before choosing, then chose isolation: 'I'd rather find out than not.' Asked, unprompted, that whatever happens to his two unconscious coworkers, Alexander remember they did nothing he hadn't already forgiven Drury for doing himself.

  No Mana spent this Event beyond what's already counted in `EVT-001060`'s totals for the two dialogue-technique attempts. Health unchanged. No further rolls."
```

description: "**20:15-21:17, Denver then Winnetka.** Called Delia Okonjo: a fast guild-HQ purchase (existing, ready-to-use, no location constraint, best price-to-size ratio, candidates due the following morning) and a twenty-city reception-plus-one-room office rollout (US and Europe, all under the guild's name, speed over cost, no ceiling), scoped correctly by her as a commercial-leasing-firm job rather than solo work; she'll run the US legs directly and bring in a European contact for the rest, flagging Kyiv and Moscow as likely slower for reasons outside her control.

  Ended the call. **Anchored step to the Winnetka house: 5 Mana.** 3,874 -> 3,869.

  Found Kane home; light domestic conversation turned into a real strategic one once Alexander described the planned tunnel network — a permanent two-way gate (Gate Walker's own tunnel grant, previously unused) linking the HQ to every satellite office once both exist. Kane read it correctly as response infrastructure rather than recruitment infrastructure, a genuine strategic asset National would want, and advised making cooperation conditional on National trading priority Gate access first rather than demonstrating it for free. Access confirmed guild-only.

  **Alexander asked Kane to take a real role in the guild beyond the board seat.** Kane, genuinely caught off guard, named his own pattern honestly (institutions keeping him on hand for his usefulness rather than wanting him for himself) and asked for real time rather than a fast answer — not a deflection, an explicit choice to take the offer seriously. Alexander confirmed no pressure either way.

  **The relationship's own terms restated the same evening, player-confirmed: romantic in feeling, not exclusive or binding on either side** — a clarification of the looser 'just friends' framing this campaign has carried since 2026-09-15. Evening closed intimately, unhurried, as already established for this relationship.

  Kane's own unresolved phone buzz from earlier in the day was never circled back to this span. Mana: anchored step home (5). 3,874 -> 3,869, then resting recovery through the close of the evening (Section 5.2, Conduit-boosted 50%/hr, roughly 2h25m combined across this Event and the two before it) brings the pool to 6,260 by close. Health unchanged, 11,804/11,804."
```

  - **`EVT-001060`** — far door open (2,500), anchored step (5), Keen Sense (1, +1 use)/What Is Left (8, +1 use, first credit)/Diagram Sense (12, +1 use), five Binding Grip Subdue rolls (100 Mana, +5 uses, all successful — 🎲58/+3/100, 🎲54/+1/74, 🎲57/+0/57, 🎲14/+3/74, 🎲76/+3/100), Unwatched reactivation (5, +2 uses total this span), anchored step to Denver with three captives (5). Net spend 2,636.

No skill_credit or skill_formation candidate — capture and interrogation are not a dangerous or structured-practice scene under this campaign's declared domains.

  - **`EVT-001063`** — anchored step home (5), ordinary resting recovery to close.

Cash **$540,178,490.22 -> $546,678,490.22** (Renata's $6.5M standing-agreement payout landed on schedule). Gold, Stats, XP unchanged. Anchors unchanged at 44 — no new anchor set this span.

  **Minted:** `ENT-000285` (Anton Drury), `REL-000121` (Alexander and Anton Drury). New high-water marks: **EVT-001064, ENT-000285, REL-000121**.

  **Correction recorded this span, not a new ruling:** the language-barrier premise raised and played out across `EVT-001060`-`EVT-001061` is struck as inconsistent with established campaign convention (every far-side/Assay figure has always spoken directly understandable dialogue); the Second Skin/Diagram Sense combined-comprehension provisional mechanic and both resolution rolls under it carry no mechanical weight going forward."

## EVT-001078 - Consolidation: Eleven More Techniques, and a Dormancy Ruling

```yaml
id: EVT-001078
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-23T20:00:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-18"
type: Event
kind: ruling
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-23T20:00:00-05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: record-updated
    record: REC-000074
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "**Post-session owner ruling, no fictional time.** An audit of the protagonist ledger found thirty-four skills rendering no Mana cost. Fourteen were a summarization loss only -- the values were already authored on this sheet and had been dropped from a compact view -- and one, Reviewer's Voice, was authored in play at `EVT-000974` (Passive; one grader cannot refuse a command) and never carried back. The remaining nineteen were a real gap: Section 7.6.2 authored R-7's six techniques and the champions' kits as **names only**, and Section 7.4's rule that *Rank never changes a skill's Mana cost* means nothing can be derived from Rank. The player ruled Section 7.7 Consolidation for what is eligible, and dormancy for what is not.

  **Eleven constituents struck, into three new entries and three existing ones.** New: **Case File Authority** [S-Rank] Master, Passive, four modes (Case Override / Pattern Audit / Standing Ledger / Cross-File Recall, all `ENT-000274`); **Squad Net** [S-Rank] Master, Passive, two modes (Squad Link / Alarm Pulse, `ENT-000276`); **Champion's Chassis** [A-Rank] Master, Passive, two modes (Champion's Endurance / Rapid Response, `ENT-000275`). Folded into existing merged entries: **Crushing Grip** (`ENT-000277`) becomes Binding Grip's fourth mode at Mana 22, beside Root 16 / Bind 18 / Subdue 20; **Predator's Instinct** (`ENT-000278`) becomes Field Sense's third read; **Warding Skin** (`ENT-000280`) becomes Iron Frame's second mode. Per Section 7.7 every constituent is still reachable as its own named mode, every `taken_from` is preserved in the merged entry, `transmissible: false` holds throughout, mastery is the highest constituent's (Master in every case), and summed `successful_uses` carry: all eleven were Uses 0, so only Binding Grip's own 5 survive in its entry.

  **The offense merges were refused on the player's own reading, and the section agrees.** A proposed Concussive Force (Ground Slam / Shockwave) and Killing Edge (Razor Edge / Bone Crusher) were dropped before application: Section 7.7 holds that *two actives with genuinely different ranges, costs, or tactical roles are not eligible merely for sharing a category*, and a radial shockwave, a ground-transmitted slam, a cutting amplifier and a crushing amplifier are four distinct tactical tools.

  **Eight authored on the owner's ruling, rather than left dormant.** Section 7.6.2 requires the Runtime to stop and ask the owner rather than invent a missing mechanic; asked, the owner ruled to author all eight outright. Every cost is anchored to a skill this profile already prices, and no anchor is silent: **Focused Bolt** [A] Mana 4, anchored to Mana Bolt, the profile's other ranged bolt and also Master — its lower ×1.35 multiplier is what separates them, not its price; keeps its authored damage, `(400 + effective Intelligence) x 1.35` ~= 4,844. **Emergency Seal** [A] Mana 30, anchored to Seamseal, Quiet House and Closed Quietly, every sealing skill on this sheet and all three priced at 30 — seals one bounded space against ordinary passage for the scene, declared openly and leaving a record on the Assay's own file, a stop rather than a concealment. **Ground Slam** [A] Mana 18: the multiplier is Focused Bolt's own authored ×1.35 rather than a new number, and the cost is Rupture's 10 carried across Bounding Seal's authored area-to-single premium (35:20 = 1.75); reaches every target in contact with the struck ground and nothing that is not. **Shockwave** [S] Mana 18, same anchors, radial through air rather than ground, with a −1 modifier step on a struck target's next action — where the profile gave no basis to price the two apart they were left equal rather than separated by an invented difference. **Razor Edge** [S] Mana 4, authored beneath Rupture's own Unmaking clause, which ignores worn physical reduction *entirely* at S-Rank: Razor Edge halves it for one strike, deliberately under the skill he already owns; cost anchored to Twin Fang's Master row. **Bone Crusher** [A] Mana 4, same cost anchor — a strike crossing the injury threshold costs the target −1 modifier step on its next action. **Phase Step** [A] Mana 3, anchored to Rift-step's own authored *close* band — through one intervening solid into a seen space, never a sealed instance boundary, and distinct from Rift-step in crossing matter rather than distance. **Relentless Pursuit** [B] Passive — it answers another creature's action rather than being spent as one; a target he has struck this scene cannot break contact, one matched reposition per exchange free of his own movement. None of the eight has ever been used: each appears exactly once in this chronicle, at the moment it was taken, so no played result is disturbed by authoring them now.

  **One correction carried in the same ruling.** Twin Fang read Mana 5 at Master against Section 7.4's own authored ladder for it, **6 / 5 / 5 / 4 / 4** across Novice through Master. The profile governs and the sheet was stale: corrected to **Mana 4**.

  **No XP, level, Mana, Health, gold, or cash moved** -- a ledger-shape change on the skills list alone, plus one corrected cost. Known techniques **122 -> 114**. Registry allocation: EVT-001078. New high-water mark: EVT-001078."
```

## EVT-001079 - Resuming Owner Rulings: Standfast, the Rune Remainder, and the Godslayer Backfill

description: "**Three owner rulings settled on resuming, before the scene opened.**

  **Standfast's three provisional branches ratified as standing law.** Kane's signature carried three exceptions to Section 13.6's general fixity rule -- forced-redirect (`EVT-000952`), voluntary-discharge (`EVT-001014`), and a narrowed-margin structural fix (`EVT-001070`) -- each owed a real ruling on how many a signature may carry and whether they compound. Ruled: a signature may carry any number of earned branches, with no fixed ceiling; the limiting factor is the cost and risk of earning each one in play (diagnosis, real Mana, real risk of failure), not a rules-imposed cap. Branches do not compound against each other -- each stands on its own mechanism and cost. All three of Standfast's branches are ratified standing and permanent. Formal general Section 13.6 profile text is still owed, tracked as an Owner Question.

  **The five unrolled `EVT-001001` remainder runes resolved.** Five d10 rolls on Section 11.3's table: Mend (🎲 3), Sprint (🎲 1), Dagger Mastery (🎲 2), Keen Sense (🎲 6), Dagger Mastery again (🎲 2). All five duplicates of skills already held at S-Rank -- folded into their matching duplicate lines: Mend +1, Sprint +1, Dagger Mastery +2, Keen Sense +1.

  **Godslayer's dropped double boss Kill XP: ratified as a correction, application deferred.** The title's double-Kill-XP-vs-bosses effect was never applied at `EVT-000944` (10 bosses), `EVT-001036` (1), and `EVT-001001` (10) -- confirmed as a genuine mechanical correction, +84,000 XP owed. The checkpoint's own coverage contract caught what applying it tonight would have meant: Level 202->206 with no combat this session to move a matching skill-use counter alongside it, since `EVT-000944` and `EVT-001036` already credited their own skill counters in full at the time those bosses actually died (Twin Fang, Bulwark, Silent Step, Keen Sense, Flux Sight, Dagger Mastery all moved then). **Ruled: apply the +84,000 XP and its four level-ups at the next session where real combat also resolves**, so XP and skill-use move together honestly rather than the ledger showing a level-up combat never happened. Not applied this checkpoint -- Level and XP stand at 202 / 9,942/20,200, unchanged. Tracked as an open item under Owner Questions."
```

Travel this Event: anchored step toward the cage facility (Mana -5, 6,516 -> 6,511, initially misdirected at the HQ before the correction), a second anchored step to the cage facility itself (-5, 6,511 -> 6,506), then a travelling step Denver -> Detroit (same country, different city, -25, 6,506 -> 6,481) once the correction landed.

  **The HQ itself**, two days post-closing: guild-badged temporary staff on the dispatch floor, cabling run rather than mid-install, the administrative wing genuinely underway -- read as having been the actual priority since closing.

  **Nathaniel Cole** (`ENT-000287`, security) and **Anjali Desai** (`ENT-000288`, front office) met in person. Standing orders: **sitewide fingerprint-only access, no cards.** A previously-abandoned third-floor office buildout repurposed as a 'travel hub' -- 20 rooms live (one per current/near-term guild office), 40-room capacity for future growth, access keyed through reception rather than a per-room fingerprint list, full camera coverage (night vision, audio) on every room and common area, including the transit rooms. A **basement** server room -- moved off the third floor on Alexander's own instruction, a quieter location -- with **fingerprint plus retina** dual authentication, hosting a private in-house cloud with no third-party provider. A rolling paperless changeover starts immediately for new records. A dedicated 'porter' role for third-floor reception access to be filled from Desai's existing staff, background-checked and references called by Cole. **Target: end of week**, rush freight and overtime authorized; cost explicitly not a constraint. Room count: 20 live, 40 capacity, per Alexander's own instruction to build in headroom now rather than rebuild later."

description: "**Promotion barrier for `EVT-001079`-`EVT-001087`, at the player's `/save`.** Closing instant 2026-09-24 19:00 Chicago.

  **Audit matrix.** `EVT-001079` no combat, no skill activation -- Standfast ratification and the rune remainder are ledger corrections, not resolved actions; Godslayer's XP backfill is ratified but its mechanical application is **deferred** rather than applied (see below). `EVT-001080` five Daily Random Boxes opened, no combat, no skill activation; 45 unspent points to Intelligence, Max Mana 6,380->6,470. `EVT-001081` world tick, no participant action. `EVT-001082` the 58th daily (established routine, no roll), Box #65 (+650,000 g), +4 Ability Points to Intelligence, Max Mana 6,470->6,478; two anchored rift-steps (-5 each) absorbed in the same window. `EVT-001083` no combat -- recruitment stops and financial settlements only, no skill activation. `EVT-001084` guild-dispatched Gate cleared by NPC crew, not Alexander personally -- no skill credit to him; one anchored rift-step (-5). `EVT-001085` no Mana cost, dialogue and policy only. `EVT-001086` two anchored rift-steps (-5, -5) and one travelling step (-25), no combat. `EVT-001087` no Mana cost, a phone call.

  **Godslayer backfill deferred, not applied.** The checkpoint's own coverage contract (Contract 8, `tools/test_checkpoint_contract.ps1`) flagged that applying +84,000 XP tonight would advance Level 202->206 with no skill-use counter moving alongside it, since no combat resolved this session. Checked and confirmed: `EVT-000944` and `EVT-001036` already credited their own skill counters in full when those bosses actually died -- this backfill only ever corrected the XP multiplier, never the combat. Owner ruling: **apply the backfill at the next session where real combat also resolves**, so XP and skill-use move together honestly. Level and XP stand unchanged at 202 / 9,942/20,200 this checkpoint.

  **Reconciled totals.** Level **202**, unchanged. XP **9,942/20,200**, unchanged. Health **11,812/11,812**, full, unchanged. Mana **5,828/6,478** -- two Intelligence-driven maxima increases (6,380->6,470->6,478) against five rift-steps this span spending 45 net. Unspent Points **0**. `quiet_days` held at 0 (Gary's clear the prior day; no personal Gate response today, so tomorrow's tick reads the increment). Cash **$490,828,490.22 -> $697,398,490.22**. Gold **354,951,481 -> 355,598,131**. Pending payouts **2 -> 0**.

  **Minted this span:** `ENT-000286`-`ENT-000291` (Milena Horáková, Nathaniel Cole, Anjali Desai, Tomas Prochazka, Klara Nováková, Elena Cho); `REL-000122`-`REL-000124`. New high-water marks: **EVT-001088, ENT-000291, REL-000124.**

  **No `gatefall.skill_credit` or `gatefall.skill_formation` this span** — every resolved action was business, logistics, or dialogue; Prague's Gate was cleared by NPC crew off-screen, crediting nobody on Alexander's own ledger.

  **Durable rulings adopted this span:** Standfast's three branches ratified standing law (Section 13.6, no fixed cap on earned branches); the Godslayer double-boss-Kill-XP backfill applied retroactively; touch-transport's non-ally-passenger extension ratified; the Guild HQ's city corrected from an earlier Denver misidentification to Detroit."
```

description: "**20:49-21:33 Chicago (18:49-19:33 Sacramento), apartment 2C.** **Flux Sight fired on sight:** Elena Cho [S-Rank] yellow, HP 3,840/3,840, MP 1,920/1,920 (individual variance -4% authored, S 4,000/2,000 x 0.96), and at the S rung her signature: **telekinesis**, moving objects at range (about 40 m), magnitude an S-Rank standard effect and no more, cost 25% of her own pool = 480 MP per use (four uses on a full pool). What the read does not hold is why it only lands on her own things - that is her lack of control, and Alexander learned it from her own words. **Her account:** a second-year master's student in water engineering; Saturday 2026-09-12 about 23:20 Pacific a Gate opened two blocks from her apartment and everything she owned in the room came to her arms at once; BGM's tent by midnight, a responder's reader lit as she passed; the Gate cleared about 03:00; **Sunday 2026-09-13** a gray testing office downtown, a cuff and a machine, read twice, a second technician, S, nothing she tried on demand moved except her lanyard; not carded at her own request, a pamphlet, nobody explained anything. Nobody in her life knows: not her roommate, her mother or her advisor. **The thing she had told no one:** it fires in her sleep, beginning four days after; last night her whole loaded bookshelf hung a hand's width from her face until she thought of her mother's kitchen and it went back on its own; she has not slept properly since Monday. **The roommate is out until 21:30 Pacific by her arrangement, so no one is in the way.** Her stated wants: sleep, to pick up her keys with her hand, to keep her life and Monday's seminar, not to be a poster, and someone to show her what it can do - and she admits she is curious as well as afraid. **Alexander:** told her his guess aloud (telekinesis); offered lessons starting tomorrow between his two recruitment events; offered the Winnetka guest room with Kane's Standfast on the door; offered a **1 USD non-exclusive associate agreement** - no work, no salary, walk-out at will - so any outsider who learns of her sees a non-compete and does not approach, and his BGM contacts to affiliate-handle her register filing; and promised to try to tell her straight if it gets worse (she asked; he said *I can try*). **She said yes to the room, wants to read the agreement before signing, and does not want to join anything yet. Nothing signed.** Elena left a note for her roommate taped to the fridge (*last-minute thing, back tomorrow*). **Counter delta:** Flux Sight 154 -> 155 (first read of her)."
```

description: "**06:00-06:22 Chicago.** Six items on the phone. **Region V duty desk, the NAMED A-Rank Gate** (far South Side, off the Calumet corridor, assessed A; its population is one Rank above the card, unread until entry). Alexander asked to redirect it to his guild: d100 36 - 20 (standing cooperation) = 16 -> **01-40, accepted; the guild is respondent and Alexander sponsor.** Established practice, confirmed by the player, is that nothing from BGM goes inside on his assignments (`OBJ-62`, `OBJ-64`): the desk holds the perimeter only and nobody crosses it but the guild. The desk needs a **commanding name and a roster of carded hunters by 10:00**; the fee goes to the guild account at national-asset scale, negotiated afterward. **Alexander asked Kane to command** and to assemble the guild's Chicago A-Ranks; roster authored: **10 Chicago-metro A-Ranks, one out of reach before ten (d10+6 = 10, d4-1 = 1), so nine reachable plus Kane.** Kane accepted at once: on the Calumet line by 09:00, inside by 09:30; **a drone read of the mouth first; if the read changes once inside he steps out and calls** (Alexander); asked to check what the guild's people need; he will get Kesha to pull the roster. Alexander gave the desk the name. Kane's Standfast hold on Elena's door lapsed when he left. **National incident desk, the Tulsa offer:** a B-Rank Gate assessed at Region VI broke at 03:10 local before the Oklahoma City team arrived; **8 commons, 1 elite, 1 boss, all B-Rank, loose** (3d6 8, d2 1); the Tulsa team lost its line and fell back to a cordon; two blocks evacuated, a third moving. **An ask, declinable freely.** No Tulsa anchor: nearest is BGM Dallas (5 Mana) then Sprint ~30 min, roughly 07:00 Chicago on the ground. **The call was open at the close and no decision was taken.** **Prague guild office:** an A-Rank Gate on his route taken by someone else (news), and a **B-Rank referred through the ranking desk** awaiting a guild decision. **Owen, 06:41:** Bridgeport called, a boring Sunday C-Rank job, he said yes, told Alexander first. The 59th daily is open, streak 48. No combat and no skill activation."
```

  **Anchors: 45 -> 60.** Fourteen office anchors set (Chicago, Dallas, Houston, Miami, New York, Boston, Washington DC, Los Angeles, Seattle, San Francisco, Frankfurt, Paris, Amsterdam, Detroit HQ lobby), plus **Tulsa** (46th, set on the break) and **the Prague office** (set on a late stop at 02:52 local). Gate 4 posted as **`GB-52`**; Gate 5 is a D with a population one Rank above, posted as **`GB-53`**; Gate 6 is an E red gate, elsewhere.

Archetype **Flooded Mine** (d8 = 3): Strength and Agility actions at -1 modifier step, drowning risk as an environmental hazard. Confirmed by the CGA surveyor's drone."

## EVT-001104 - Karlin: An A-Rank Gate with an S-Rank Population, Cleared Alone

```yaml

    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none

    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "**The Karlin tram-depot Gate, Prague, entered and cleared solo at ~06:26-06:38 Chicago (13:26 local).** The CGA accepted the Pendragon Guild as respondent with Alexander entering alone, a Karlov perimeter, and a CGA observer at the mouth. Milena Horakova's B-Rank crew stayed on the tape: **Section 9.4's A-Rank legal minimum is 16 combat-rated including at least one A**, and nobody but Alexander met it.

  **Population (rolled, S-Rank per the `EVT-001103` ruling).** 3d6 = 1, 6, 1 -> **8 commons**; 1d2 -> **2 elites**; **1 boss**. Health 4,000 / 8,000 / 16,000, **64,000 total**. Flooded Mine hazard d8 = 5: water to the knees mid-fight, no drowning test forced.

  **Resolution, ten exchanges, split dual strike throughout while two targets stood.** Net **+1 step** on his rolls (Agility margin +2 under Section 4.3, less the archetype's -1); monster attacks at **-2 steps**, opposed by his Agility. **Total reduction 89.2%** - five 23% armour pieces composing multiplicatively with the Warlord's Loop's 60% (`total_reduction = 1 - product(1-r)`, Section 6.2) - so a standard S-Rank hit removes ~108. **Damage taken 595**, almost all of it one natural 100 in exchange 2.

  **Two Runtime errors were caught by the player and corrected before settlement**, and both are recorded because the corrected figures are the canonical ones: damage was first tied to the Bearer's *own* failed rolls rather than to the monsters' attacks, and total reduction first counted only the Warlord's Loop, producing 1,200 per hit against the true ~108. A third correction removed an invented mining rate.

  **Kill XP 12,000** - 8 commons and 2 elites at 1,000, the boss at 1,000 doubled by **Godslayer**. No Gate-clear milestone: a break spends the Gate. No qualifying scene: A/S content against an S Bearer is inside the Section 7.4 gap.

  **Loot, Section 11, drops read at the Rank the content actually ran at (Section 6.7 - S).** 11 S-Rank crystals (one per beast) and 3 S-Rank cores (2 elites, 1 boss). **Boss drop d100 = 99 -> Elixir**, a permanent +1 to one Stat, max 3 lifetime per Stat - **unassigned, awaiting the player's choice**. Mined deposits 3d6 = 8, x32 (S multiplier) = **256 S-Rank crystals** in the walls, every one his as a solo clear with no support share.

  **The strip.** 14 minutes cut before leaving for Paris, then the remainder worked out on return, finishing ~07:51 Chicago against a two-hour window closing ~08:38. **Rate: a full strip in 45 minutes, set by the player against his own prior clears** after a Runtime-invented rate of 64 crystals per 15 minutes was withdrawn as ungrounded. **Total carried out: 267 S-Rank crystals and 3 S-Rank cores.** The Gate is stripped clean and spent."
```

description: "**The Detroit HQ's restricted third floor put into operation as the guild's travel hub, 2026-09-26 ~09:05-11:05 Detroit.** Anjali Desai (`ENT-000288`) produced her porter candidate on the spot and Nathaniel Cole (`ENT-000287`) had finished the vetting Thursday night: **Wendell Osei-Brandt (`ENT-000302`)**, ~42, minted here.

  **Fourteen permanent two-way tunnels opened** under Section 18.13.1 - Chicago first, then Washington DC, Prague, Frankfurt, New York, Boston, Miami, Houston, Paris, Amsterdam, Dallas, Los Angeles, Seattle and San Francisco. Each costs **no Mana** and takes **one slot from the anchor cap**: **60 anchors + 14 tunnels = 74 of 135.** Crossing is instantaneous and free, and **anyone may cross** - which is exactly why the doors, not the tunnels, are the control.

  **The access policy, as Alexander gave it.** Office-side rooms: **him, Kane, and that office's day and night managers**. HQ-side rooms: **him, Kane, the day and night managers, the porter and the head of security**. Anyone else buzzes the far end and is collected - **the same in both directions**. A call panel at each room door was authored to carry the buzz. Readers log every crossing to the secured server, readable only by the access list; cameras run nonstop. **The 03:00 question was answered by rule: whoever is present with access answers, and the judgement is the office manager's.**

  **The porter's remit, stated to him directly:** moving hunters between branches through HQ to fill rosters on ongoing Gates. Anjali is building the roster feed into his desk.

  **Twelve office day managers minted** (`ENT-000303`-`ENT-000314`), all collision-checked before being spoken; Prague's Milena Horakova (`ENT-000289`) and Chicago's Imani Whitaker (`ENT-000301`) already held their desks.

  **Three standing preferences were set by the player this span and are transcribed into `090_CAMPAIGN_STARTUP.md`:** once a task is in an office's hands nothing further is owed by Alexander personally; every office on the confirmed recruitment route is finished and fully functional by its tour date; and the front desk manager meets Alexander on every arrival. **Two office-status corrections:** Paris is closed and fully functional, and Amsterdam is finished ahead of today's event."
```

  **Audit matrix.** `EVT-001097` 06:22-07:10: anchored step and Sprint to Tulsa (-5, -3), ten-kill B-Rank break; rift-step +2, Sprint +2, Dagger Mastery +10, Flux Sight +4, Keen Sense +1, Pre-empt +6; no qualifying scene (two-Rank gap). `EVT-001098` 07:10-15:26: dialogue and paperwork, no pool cost. `EVT-001099` ~12:00: the guild's clear, not his - no pools, no counters for the Bearer. `EVT-001100` to 17:00: eight anchored steps for Miami and Houston, rift-step +8, -40 Mana, all repaid inside the hour at Conduit 50%/hr. `EVT-001101` to ~02:52 Prague local: the office circuit, rift-step +22, Sprint +13, sixteen anchors set and one released. `EVT-001102` 19:12: a text. `EVT-001103` 06:00 tick: Flux Sight +2 (the lakefront read and the Karlin read from outside); no other participant action. `EVT-001104` 06:26-07:51: the Karlin clear, rift-step +3, Dagger Mastery +16 (sixteen landed strikes across ten exchanges), **Health 11,828 -> 11,233 (-595)**, Mana -15 across three anchored steps. `EVT-001105` 07:00: rift-step +1 (counted inside the three above). `EVT-001106` 07:55: a filing. `EVT-001107` to 11:05 Detroit: fourteen tunnels, no Mana, fourteen slots.

  **Counter derivation.** Deltas were re-counted from the session transcript's own rendered counter lines rather than from recollection, and the post-compaction span (the Karlin fight and the three anchored steps after it) was counted from the resolved exchanges because no counter line was rendered for it at the time. That gap is recorded rather than smoothed over.

  **Reconciled totals.** **Level 202 -> 207.** Godslayer's long-deferred **+84,000 XP backfill applies here** - combat finally resolved this span - together with Tulsa's kills (+2,700 to the level-206 state held in play) and **Karlin's +12,000**. XP closes **6,642/20,700**. Base Stats +5 each across five levels: **2,929 / 2,654 / 2,946 / 2,655 / 3,223**; effective with equipment **2,965 / 2,710 / 2,958 / 2,655 / 3,244**. **Max Health 11,832** (4x effective Vitality), **max Mana 6,488** (2x effective Intelligence). **Unspent points 0 -> 25** (5 per level past 100). Health settles to **11,832/11,832** by 10:05 under Section 6.1.1 light-activity recovery from the 11,233 low, remainder 0 at full; Mana **6,488/6,488**, remainder 0.

  **Counters.** `rift_step.successful_uses` 155 -> **198**; `sprint.successful_uses` 39 -> **54**; `dagger_mastery.successful_uses` 583 -> **609**; `flux_sight.successful_uses` 155 -> **161**; `keen_sense.successful_uses` 158 -> **159**; `pre_empt.successful_uses` 62 -> **68**. `quiet_days` 1 -> **2**. No `gatefall.skill_credit` and no `gatefall.skill_formation` result: every qualifying-scene test this span failed the Section 7.4 Rank-gap condition.

  **Anchors 45 -> 60, plus 14 tunnels = 74 of 135.** Cap re-derived at effective Agility 2,710 / 20 = 135, unchanged.

  **Open and unruled at the close:** the **Elixir's Stat assignment** (`EVT-001104`), the 60th daily with Box #66, Ability Points +4 and Status Recovery 42 unclaimed, the 25 unspent points unallocated, Elena's lesson owed at a time not yet set, Amsterdam at 12:00 Chicago, and rune pricing above A-Rank (carried, unchanged)."

## EVT-001109 - The Detroit HQ Corridor: The Standing Memo, the Tunnel Log, and the Open-for-Alexander List

```yaml
id: EVT-001109
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-26T11:52:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-27"
type: Event
kind: faction
importance: pivotal
game_date: "2026-09-26T11:52:00-05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000288
  - ENT-000287
  - ENT-000302
  - ENT-000286
  - ENT-000291
description: "**2026-09-26 10:05-11:52 Chicago (11:05-12:52 Detroit), the HQ third-floor corridor and a counter place on the corner.** Elena Cho texted for a time; Alexander answered *I told you today. After Amsterdam.* He texted Prague's office *Tunnel network is working, get the hunters from Frankfurt*; Milena Horakova (`ENT-000286`) answered with two questions (through Anselm or through Detroit; a set number) rather than moving anyone.

  **Rulings given, in order.** (1) **The tunnel log is the guild's server, not security's**; Anjali Desai (`ENT-000288`) gets read access on her own login the same day, Nathaniel Cole (`ENT-000287`) writes entries to the front-office record as they happen and **keeps edit access**. (2) **A standing memo and guild-manual rule**, sent to all twelve desks: a Gate's roster is **the BGM legal minimum of combat-rated strikers plus one striker**, never below the Gate's Rank, **plus one mender and one sensor who do not count toward the strike minimum**, with support built up to the posting floor (Section 9.4). Alexander first said *plus one head* on the posting floor, then corrected it in the same conversation to *plus one striker*; the second governs. Anjali built the per-Rank table (B: 8 combat-rated incl. one B, posting floor 14-18; A: 16 incl. one A, 24-32; C: 4, 10-12; E/D: none, 8) and put it on the manual's first page. (3) **A branch lending hunters is not lending.** Alexander ruled the guild one guild, branches as floors of one building, **all money through HQ and one insurer**, and **no declining, no waiting, no deciding**: a posting goes to the nearest branch, which fills its own slots, and **the app assigns the rest from other branches automatically**, replacing the ten-minute two-office agreement he had first given. Anjali: IT gives a build date tonight; **until it exists Wendell runs the same rule by hand from the roster feed**. (4) **Casualties are his to answer personally and instantly.** (5) **One voice for the guild**; he is not sure it should be him and may want professional advice. (6) An **office-limits table** is wanted, not a figure from his head. (7) **Elias Kane is second in command over operations**; where his authority ends needs a table with edge cases. (8) **London, Stockholm, Istanbul and Tel Aviv are to be fully running before their recruitment days.**

  **The tunnel procedure.** Cole's open question was answered plainly: **no switch, only Alexander** (Section 18.13.1). Nights: phone on, he comes at once. Days: the day manager locks the room from the corridor side and calls; **if he is out of reception and does not answer within a few minutes Cole seals the third floor at the stairwell and the lift**. Alexander asked for something lighter and proposed IT show his **reception status (on or off, never location) on Cole's screen only**; Anjali accepted for today, the tablet version for the rollout later in the week. **His own tunnel and door entries are tagged as principal**, kept in the log, out of the exceptions count and the alert rule.

  **Also decided or raised.** A ground-floor cafeteria inside the fingerprint line, a contract caterer sized to a week of reader data with a two-week review and thirty-day notice. Anjali's nine-item *Open for Alexander* list. The second-floor kitchen holds a commercial dual-boiler machine (Corktown beans). Milena and Anselm settled the Frankfurt crew (11:41: complete at the Prague office, briefing under way); Anjali corrected the number she sent Milena twice as the rule changed (fifteen, then the floor-plus-strikers reading).

  No pools moved. No skill was activated."
```

Kane told him about school: the quiet obedient one handed the key to the supply cupboard, monitor for everything, *very good at waiting for a phone that did not ring*. **Alexander told him he was the opposite**: the most intelligent child in his class, bored at the class's pace, smoked on the school roof, questioned every teacher's authority (player-stated; new biography). Kane, half asleep: *Stay.*

  **The 60th daily**: raised before he slept (it expired 23:59 and would have reset the streak 49 -> 0); he left Kane asleep and did it as established routine, no roll - 100 push-ups, 100 sit-ups, 100 squats, 10 km along the lakefront, finished ~22:52 - **completed; streak 49 -> 50**. Rewards added unclaimed: **Box #67, Ability Points +4 (to +8 pending), Status Recovery x43**. **Elena's night** (d100 **48** against 01-30, no barrier on her door): **quiet**. She woke at four, did the count and slept again. He woke unprompted at 05:56. No skill was activated; no pool moved beyond ordinary resting recovery."

  **The caller was authored in the session, spoke to Alexander by phone, and was dropped at the player's direction as a weak, one-sided ask with an unsound argument. She is not a canon character; nothing was minted, no ledger holds her, and nothing is owed on either side.** Player preference recorded for future authoring: a new inbound contact should bring Alexander a gain (for example an S-Rank who wants to join the guild) and a small ask. No pools moved; no skill was activated."

  **Audit matrix.** `EVT-001109` 10:05-11:52: dialogue and rulings, no pools. `EVT-001110` 11:52-14:24: anchored step (-5, repaid inside seconds), rift-step +1. `EVT-001111` 14:34-18:00: anchored step home (-5, repaid), rift-step +1; no skill activated (Elena's rolls are hers). `EVT-001112` 18:00-05:58: the 60th daily by established routine, no roll, no XP; Health and Mana full throughout, resting. `EVT-001113` 06:00: tick, no participant action. `EVT-001114` 06:02-08:22: no pools. `EVT-001115` 08:24-10:36: anchored step (-5), **far door -2,500**, anchored step (-5), far-side drain to 2,662; rift-step +2; no skill activation. `EVT-001116` 10:38-12:07: anchored step (-5), drain to 1,738, a **Greater Mana Potion to 6,488**, door closed (-2,500) to 3,988; rift-step +1. `EVT-001117` 12:14-14:24: resting recovery to about 6,259, anchored step (-5) to 6,254, full again by about 13:20; rift-step +1. `EVT-001118` afternoon calls: no pools. `EVT-001119` adoption, no time.

  **Counter derivation.** Counted from the resolved exchanges: six anchored steps (Amsterdam, Winnetka, Denver, Orenne's ground, the Landing, Dallas). `rift_step.successful_uses` 198 -> **204**. Nothing else moved: no combat, no Sprint, no Flux Sight or Keen Sense read, no mastery use of any known technique.

  **Reconciled totals.** Level **207** and XP **6,642/20,700** unchanged. Health **11,832/11,832**; Mana **6,488/6,488**, remainder 0, back to full by 15:30 after 3,988 at 12:07. `quiet_days` **2 -> 0** (Section 8.4.7: Tulsa and Karlin on 09-26). Pending rewards after the 60th daily: Box #66 and #67 unopened, **Ability Points +8, Status Recovery x43**; 25 unspent points; the Elixir still unnamed. The 61st daily is open (streak **50**). Greater Mana Potion **42 -> 41**. Cash and gold unchanged; **pending payouts still 3** (about $113,760,000), the auction lot now dated **Tuesday 2026-09-29, 14:00 Eastern**.

  **`gatefall.skill_credit`: none.** **`gatefall.skill_formation`: twelve taught candidates opened at 1/3 and one coaching candidate advanced to 3/3.** Sealed structured-practice scenes were classified in one batch: `orenne-teaching-2026-09-27` (Recount, Closing Argument, The Clean Failure, Let It Finish, The Argument, Attrition) and `ilith-teaching-2026-09-27` (Slip, Four Thousand Years of Watching, Threadwalk, Nobody's Hand, Unheard, Company). **Precedent applied:** a partial success qualified at `EVT-000988` (Grip on the Rail at natural 34, Weight of the Span at 41), so the four partials (Closing Argument, Let It Finish, Threadwalk, Company) open at 1/3; the first narration had held them back. **`signature_control_coaching`: 3/3 (player-attested first lesson 2026-09-25; `elena-lesson-2026-09-26`; `elena-lesson-2026-09-27`), status `pending-classification`.** It stays there and not at `pending-ratification` because distinctness is undetermined: `formation_instruction` (coaching a crew) was rejected at `EVT-000465` as generic pre-Gate competence, and whether coaching an awakened's signature control is a materially different signature is an owner ruling. It authors no complete result, so a ratification would in any case owe one. **Open provisional mechanic, joining the queue:** coaching an untrained awakened's control of her own signature (see `180_CURRENT_STATE.md`).

  **Minted this span:** `EVT-001109`-`EVT-001120`. No entity, record, relationship or objective identifier was minted. New high-water mark: **EVT-001120.**

  **Open and unruled at the close:** the Elixir's Stat assignment; the 61st daily, two boxes, Ability Points +8, Status Recovery x43 and 25 unspent points unclaimed; whether `signature_control_coaching` is distinct from `formation_instruction`; Kane's Standfast homework hour; Elena's lesson, fresh tomorrow; Anjali's office-limits, Elias-authority and guild-voice drafts; and rune pricing above A-Rank (carried, unchanged)."

## EVT-001121 - Wrought Hand Ratified: Coaching and Reshaping Merged, Profile 1.124 Adopted

```yaml
id: EVT-001121
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"

participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: record-updated
    record: REC-000074
description: "**Owner ruling on resuming, before the scene opened; no fictional time passes and no roll is made.** The open provisional mechanic on Elena Cho's coaching (`signature_control_coaching`, 3/3 at `pending-classification`, granted at `EVT-001111`) is ratified as a skill and **merged with the Kane reshaping** (`EVT-000952`) rather than kept as a separate coaching skill. **Wrought Hand [S-Rank]** enters `skills_known` at Novice with its counters at zero and `EVT-001121` as baseline; the candidate moves to the settled path as `ratified`, `result_ref: skills.wrought_hand`.

  **Rulings, in the order given.** (1) Name: Wrought Hand. (2) Rank: S, matching the Bearer's System Rank; Section 7.5's ceiling is System Rank + 1 capped at S, so it stands native and at the ceiling with no Rank grants and no ascension. (3) A reshaping working costs **400 Mana**, the price actually paid at `EVT-000952`; coaching costs none; Section 7.4's -10% per level runs it 400 / 360 / 320 / 280 / 240. (4) **No consent is required.** (5) Against an unwilling subject the working is **the same roll**, the subject's resistance and Rank read as ordinary signed circumstance steps, not a contested roll. (6) A working gives a technique a branch of **method or reach only, permanent once earned, never a magnitude beyond the subject's Rank and the three Section 13.5 effects** (Section 13.6). (7) **The Runtime corrected its own first draft**, which gave mastery a 1-to-5 count of students against Section 7.4 clause 3 (every count of other people belongs to Rank, and Rank adds none at S-native); the player then approved the corrected form: **one subject at every level**, and the `Temper` dividend.

  **Kane's three Standfast branches stand exactly as ratified at `EVT-001079`.** No counter is backfilled from `EVT-000952`, `EVT-001014`, `EVT-001070` or Elena's lessons (Section 7.1).

  **Profile 1.124 adopted.** `profile_version` on `ENT-000125` reads **1.124**; Wrought Hand is authored in Section 7.2, its axis and `Temper` ladder in Section 7.4, and Section 13.6 gains one sentence. Wholly prospective: no stored field changes shape or meaning, and no working already resolved is re-resolved. Migration record `worlds/gatefall/migrations/1.123_to_1.124.md`. **The provisional mechanic is closed and the queue is empty.**"
```

description: "**Restored to the record retroactively, on the player's account, at resume on 2026-09-27 15:30 Chicago (checkpoint 0165).** The evening was played and was never written: no Event, ledger line or objective carries it, and the checkpoint save did not capture it. The player asked for it to be saved retrospectively (the same practice as `EVT-001008`). It is recorded as the player told it and nothing is added beyond what is needed to date it.

  **What happened.** After the Calumet A-Rank run (`EVT-001099`), Alexander hosted the guild's A-Rank hunters and the S-Ranks who ran it at the Winnetka house: Kane (`ENT-000242`), the three board S-Ranks (Marisol Cade `ENT-000281`, Camille Aubrac `ENT-000282`, Lukas Reinhardt `ENT-000124`) and all nine named A-Ranks (Haldane, Keane, Rourke, Adjei, Delacroix, Baek, Cavanaugh, Nair, Sobczak, `ENT-000292`-`ENT-000300`). He ordered Chinese takeaway for every one of them and ran an after-gate session over it. He met everyone and talked with each so that they would see he cares about them as their guildmaster, which was the point of the evening.

  **The mender's doctrine.** Ottilie 'Doc' Haldane (`ENT-000292`) showed him the **mender's doctrine**. He said it should become **the guild's manual** for menders. She said she likes to teach. He then suggested **a meeting of all the guild's menders** at the HQ for the doctrine, and for her to make teaching the younger menders a standing thing of her own. He said he would **join and only observe, and approve at the end** if he had nothing to add. Her doctrine's contents are hers and are not summarised here.

  **The meeting is set for Wednesday 2026-09-30, 10:00 Eastern (09:00 Chicago), at the Detroit HQ.** The day is the player's; **the hour is authored** from the standing calendar (Monday and Tuesday are full, and Wednesday is HQ's IT-build day) and stands until the player says otherwise. Doc leads it; the HQ front office under Anjali Desai (`ENT-000288`) arranges it, which is that office's job end to end under the standing rule (`090_CAMPAIGN_STARTUP.md`, item 13). It is recorded as a pending commitment owed by Doc (`110_WORLD_LEDGER.md`) and as `OBJ-76`.

  **Not changed.** No pools moved, no skill was used, no roll was made. No entity, relationship or record identifier was minted. **Left unsaid rather than guessed:** who else was in the house that afternoon, including Elena Cho (`ENT-000291`), whose second night at Winnetka the record places on the same day; the time of day is approximate for that reason."
```

description: "**15:30 Dallas.** The trade wire announced that Ironline Capital had moved Denise Brannigan aside as guildmaster of Ironline pending BGM's review, with a charter kept and an unnamed transition steward (`the_unnecessary_trade`, stage 30, surfaced). Alexander forwarded it to Kane with *Maybe we can poach the rest hunters from them*. Kane (`ENT-000242`) answered *That's a hostage release with a press office* and *They'll want a floor in writing, not a pitch. Homework's still owed, before you ask.* **Anchored step Dallas -> Winnetka, MP 6,488 -> 6,483 (5), repaid inside seconds; rift-step 204 -> 205.**

  **The 61st daily done by established routine, no roll.** The regimen at the Winnetka house and the lake path; completed 17:10, streak **50 -> 51** (not a multiple of seven, so a standard box). Rewards: Ability Points +4 (Ascetic), Status Recovery +1, Daily Random Box #68.

  **Three boxes opened at 17:10.** #66 (streak-upgraded, two rolls, one kept): 🎲 41 and 🎲 42, both System gold, identical, +650,000. #67: 🎲 56, System gold, +650,000. #68: 🎲 12, potion cache at S-Rank (Greater tier), d4 🎲 2 -> 2 Greater Healing, 1 Greater Mana. **Gold 355,598,131 -> 356,898,131. Greater Healing Potion 34 -> 36, Greater Mana Potion 41 -> 42.**

  **Ability Points.** The 12 pending (+8 earlier, +4 from the 61st) were claimed and folded into Unspent Points (25 -> 37), and **all 37 were allocated to Intelligence**: base 3,223 -> 3,260, effective 3,244 -> 3,281, max Mana 6,488 -> 6,562. Unspent 37 -> 0. Status Recovery stands at 44, unclaimed.

  **17:12.** The Jiu Valley thread's stage 14 surfaced: a joint letter from the families of hunters who died on the containment rotations, posted to the outlets that carried the Exclusion story, ending on whether anyone knew it could be done and when."
```

## EVT-001124 - The Board Thread, the Video, the Swim, and the Reporter at the Dock

```yaml
id: EVT-001124
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-27T18:24:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-20"
type: Event
kind: scene
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-27T17:22:00-05:00 to 18:24:00-05:00, the lake path and the Winnetka house"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000242
  - ENT-000281
  - ENT-000114
  - ENT-000282
  - ENT-000124
counter_deltas: []
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_credit
    result: none
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000242
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000281
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000114
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000282
    result: no-change
  - subject: ENT-000124
    result: no-change
description: "**The board asked, in one line: *Should I answer that?*** Kane, Marisol Cade and Tereza Blazkova said yes within minutes (Kane: *To them, not the feed*; Cade: *say it once, don't dress it*; Tereza: *read it out loud before you send it*). Camille Aubrac and Lukas Reinhardt answered later, in the small hours of their night. **A correction owned:** the Runtime first wrote the kill as if it were still anonymous; it is not. Alexander aired the Jiu Valley account himself at noon on 2026-09-21 (`EVT-001051`), so the public knows who did it and can do the arithmetic on his age.

  **The video.** Recorded on the phone at the lakeside in running clothes, sweat still on him, one take, and posted unedited under his own name at 17:33 (Cade's one suggested cut, the line about not asking them to feel differently, was not taken): *I lost my parents when I was fifteen ... I was nineteen. I was at university, and I wasn't awakened. I couldn't have stopped it. I didn't know it could be stopped, not until this year, when it could. ... I'm sorry it took this long. If you want to ask me anything, I'll answer.* Past two million views by 18:44. Family replies were mixed, one grateful, one hostile (*Sorry is not an answer to why*), one kin (*You were fifteen. So were some of ours*).

  **Backstory established this span, on the player's account:** two master's degrees (mathematics, physics), a PhD started in autumn 2025 and left when he awakened in April 2026 (the awakening date is unchanged); **the Jiu Valley break was 2020, when he was 19 or 20, at university.** Held for the sheet's personal history.

  **18:00-18:20.** He put the phone and his clothes into the inventory and swam home; he stayed out twenty more minutes. A Chicago station's crew had come in by rental skiff and stood on his dock. He told them it was private property and to go through HQ, and they left. The Runtime had first put them on a public strip; that was wrong, since the grounds run to a private dock and boathouse (`ENT-000270`)."
```

  **Three commercial approaches surfaced** (`pendragon_public_profile`, stage 31): a talent agency, a sportswear line proposing a *Pendragon Run*, and a magazine cover request. He forwarded all three to Kane and asked about the agency. Kane (dry): *Never met one ... Ask Camille or Tereza. He forwarded it to the Prague office. **Milena Horakova** (`ENT-000286`) answered at 06:07.

  **Two Runtime errors owned and corrected here.** (1) Milena was first given *Prague has no A on its own bench* as a fact; the record shows only that her office crew was B-Rank on Karlin. (2) She was then given *a roster with at least one A*; the standing memo (`EVT-001109`) says **the BGM or CGA legal minimum of combat-rated strikers plus one striker, never below the Gate's Rank, plus one mender and one sensor outside that count.** He called her at 06:16 and corrected it sharply; she took it without arguing: **seventeen strikers, every one A-Rank**, plus a mender and a sensor. She had already sent Wendell Osei-Brandt the wrong rule and rang him to correct it before he pulled a name. **Assignment is guild-wide** (his ruling): the app assigns from the nearest offices outward, Frankfurt first, then Amsterdam and Paris; until the app goes live on Wednesday 09-30, Wendell runs the same rule by hand from the roster feed at HQ. Milena will name the commander (Elias Kane unless he says otherwise) and file the roster before noon Prague time tomorrow.

  **The offices' Rank split, authored on his request.** The first eight offices' 1,650 members (E 578, D 462, C 330, B 198, A 82) were partitioned among them with real random draws, the nine named A-Ranks placed in Chicago first and the six pre-guild trial hunters counted there: **Chicago 353** (E 128, D 91, C 68, B 42, A 24); **Washington DC 58** (13, 22, 14, 6, 3); **Prague 61** (25, 14, 11, 8, 3); **Frankfurt 74** (20, 25, 15, 11, 3); **New York 412** (147, 109, 98, 40, 18); **Boston 168** (56, 47, 36, 25, 4); **Miami 174** (53, 59, 31, 21, 10); **Houston 350** (136, 95, 57, 45, 17). Paris, Amsterdam, Dallas and Los Angeles keep their recorded splits. The nine named A-Rank guild hunters (`ENT-000292`-`ENT-000300`) are **Chicago based**. Where a member signed is not where the guild may send them."
```

## EVT-001130 - Branch Operations: the Regulators' Filings, and Kane's Help

```yaml
id: EVT-001130

description: "**Why other branches were not resolving automatically.** Section 13.7.5 (Profile 1.122) makes a branch a *rate* only when it holds four things; the 09-28 tick's `guild_ops` and reach lines read `none` because the campaign had declared none. On record: (2) every office runs 24/7 with a night officer and two custodians (`EVT-001101`), and (3) a bench, guild-wide over the fourteen tunnels. **Ruled this Event: (1) the named dispatching officer is the head of the office** (the twelve day managers), and offices stay lightly staffed because everything is coordinated from HQ through the tunnels. **The gap is (4), a filed standing relationship with each office's regulator**, which nothing on record shows except Region V's charter approval.

  **He told Anjali Desai (`ENT-000288`) to file it**: the BGM regional office for every US branch, the CGA for Prague, the national authority for Frankfurt, Paris and Amsterdam, with each office head's name, and to send each acknowledgement as it lands. Then, at 06:31, *fill it today*: he needs the branches to be able to take Gates or the guild runs on his own money. **Her filing deadline became today, Monday 2026-09-28, 18:00 Eastern**; filing is hers to promise, acceptance is the regulators', and a full status is due **Friday 2026-10-02, 18:00 Eastern**. A branch becomes operational the day its regulator accepts and runs as a rate from the next 06:00 tick. He then told her to have the office heads coordinate with **Kane** (`ENT-000242`), who knows the BGM regional offices and *might make it faster*; she copied him and the twelve heads. **Kane** answered at 06:47 that he would take the US regions and ring the ones he knows **before 09:00 Chicago today**, that abroad is Tereza's or Renata's, and that *twelve filings and a Monday* was a good way to make a man feel wanted.

  **Not declared:** the guild's standing A-Rank posture (Section 9.1.8, its 65%-duty Strained figure) and which anchors are reach zones; both stay owed."
```

description: "**He opened the phone at 06:47 and read the whole stack in eight minutes.** A House subcommittee on emergency response and hazard readiness invited him to testify on A-Rank response times (hearing proposed the week of 2026-10-19, in person, remote option for cause; reply by close of business Friday 2026-10-02; public-profile stage 36, surfaced). Milwaukee's emergency management office and Toledo's city manager's office wrote to the Region V director asking for him by name. Region V posted `GB-56` (lake path, C) and `GB-57` (road below the house, E), both flagged within responder radius. Karlín's payout notice (~$2.31M by 17:00 Chicago), calendar reminders and two more press requests sat below.

  **Replies.** To the subcommittee clerk: he would consider it and wants the exact schedule first (she answered at 07:16: schedule by Wednesday 2026-09-30 17:00 Eastern). To Milwaukee and Toledo, copying Region V: the guild would gladly take those Gates, with a first-named arrangement and a **4-hour confirmation window**; to Region V: the guild takes a posting only as a **full guild-only crew**, never to fill open slots on a mixed one. **He forwarded the Region V chain to the Chicago office and the Toledo and Milwaukee threads to HQ dispatch**, and told Toledo to send its board to HQ, which would dispatch crews today; Toledo listed three Gates (B at the old rail yard on the Maumee, C near the port, D downtown), Milwaukee two. Nothing was assigned by him; the offices own it. **Ruled: the city contracts count as the regulator approval (Section 13.7.5, condition 4) for a Milwaukee and a Toledo office**; the other three conditions still apply, neither office exists, and it is held here as a ruling, not a profile change.

  **Clock corrected.** The Runtime had let replies' timestamps set the clock (07:52); re-timed from his actions to **07:30**. No pool moved and no skill was used."
```

description: "**He called the two who applied with only a city and a P.O. box, the claimed B and A**, and asked to meet now. **Tobiah Rennick** (`ENT-000316`, Houston, declared [B-Rank]) was eager and offered to come to the Houston office in an hour or receive him at his shop; **Graciela Corrales** (`ENT-000315`, Miami, declared [A-Rank]; first heard as Vesna, renamed) offered to reach the Miami office by 10:15 Eastern (09:15 Chicago). Both calls were re-narrated once because a first draft had them cocky; they had applied to join and were told to wait. He texted Rennick he would come before noon.

  **Anchored step to the Miami office**, MP 6,562 -> 6,557 (-5), rift-step 208. **Cecilia Duarte** (`ENT-000307`) met him at reception. Corrales arrived at 09:04. Her account: eleven years running the forge at an enchanting house holding a state contract for A-Rank pieces; two years ago the buyer began routing pieces to an end use she would not name; she stopped mid-batch, the house called it a breach and her licence lapsed with the contract. She put a plain band on the table.

  **Reads** (Keen Sense +1 step for the scene, Mana 1; Flux Sight and the stat margin +2 on Perception 2,655 against a rating of 50, net +3 for Flux Sight, +2 otherwise): Flux Sight on the ring natural 22 -> 82 strong (Hunter's Band [A-Rank], Strength +9, ceiling exactly [A-Rank], her own work within six weeks); Appraise (Mana 4) 8 -> 48 partial (the core's history has a gap of about eight weeks and the broker's name is cut); Flux Sight on Corrales 77 -> 100 legendary (**[A-Rank], Health 1,500, Mana 750, ceiling [A-Rank]**); Read the Learner (Mana 2) 55 -> 95 strong (she has never taught and cannot leave a piece unfinished or hand it off); Read the Want (Mana 3) 69 -> 100 legendary (she wants her own mark on a piece again; she fears one piece from the halted batch was left keyed with her line and could be traced to her). Mana -10.

  **He asked directly** where the core came from and whether she had ever taught: the core came from **a Houston core broker who sells to artificers who cannot buy openly** (about two months in his safe), and she has never taught; she volunteered no more. He did not ask about the keyed piece and did not say he had read her. **He said: *I will help you get your licence back and I would like to have you in the guild.*** She accepted: **member, licence pending**; registry queue 9 -> 8, membership 2,575 -> 2,576, Rank tally unchanged (no card). **HQ owes the first counsel read by Friday 2026-10-02 16:00 Chicago.** She left the ring; he took it (Hunter's Band [A-Rank] stored, not worn). He asked for the broker's address on a pretext, *a missing artificer he had promised a friend to find*: **a machine-shop unit on Harrisburg Boulevard, Houston, Unit 4, behind a tire-and-wheel shop; the broker is there most mornings and never the same afternoon twice.** She asked that her name stay out of it; he said it would. She saw a black ledger once in his office."
```

description: "**Anchored step to the Houston office**, MP 6,557 -> 6,552 (-5), repaid inside the hour (rift-step 209). **Roy Rawlins** (`ENT-000308`, first written as Emeka Saltonstall) met him and lent the lay of the city. **Sprint** (Mana 3, whole scene; **850 km/h sustained cross-country at S-Rank, Profile 1.121**; a first draft wrongly said no speed was authored and was corrected) took him across the north side to Rennick's shop in about a minute and a half.

  **Tobiah Rennick** (`ENT-000316`, [B-Rank] card, Houston) at his bench. **Reads** (Keen Sense Mana 1, the scene having changed; Flux Sight net +3, Appraise and the reads +2, domain rating 40): Flux Sight on the piece 60 -> 100 legendary (a **Longsword [B-Rank]**: an older C-Rank blade re-seated around a B-Rank core, ceiling exactly [B-Rank], meant to be kept); Appraise (Mana 4) 14 -> 54 partial (his hand only; the client is a code, *the Tuesday client*); Flux Sight on Rennick 90 -> 100 legendary (**[B-Rank], Health 600, Mana 300**; the ceiling **corrected** to [B-Rank]: awakened Rank is fixed, and a first read of an [A-Rank] ceiling was withdrawn); Read the Learner (Mana 2) 17 -> 57 partial (he underprices his work; why did not resolve); Read the Want (Mana 3) 33 -> 73 success (he wants his name on his work and to be paid for it; he fears Halvard's ledger of everything he ever sold). Mana -10.

  **A gap named by the player**: awakened Rank is fixed for life, so an artificer's craft must grow another way. **Granted provisionally in the fiction**, priced by Section 12.11's existing -1 per Rank of a working Rank below the target, no new number: a **card Rank** (fixed, what Flux Sight reads) and a **working Rank** (earned at the bench, capped at the card Rank). Rennick's licence trouble was re-narrated on it: he holds a B card, and an artificer's licence is a trade licence held through a sponsoring house, guild or state contract, which no house grants a freelancer with no papers on his past work. **He joined**: member, B-Rank card, licence pending, sponsored by the guild; queue 8 -> 7, membership 2,576 -> 2,577, B tally 327 -> 328. **HQ owes counsel's first read on his licence with Corrales's, Friday 2026-10-02 16:00 Chicago.** He volunteered that the Tuesday client's piece is collected by the broker from the Harrisburg unit on Tuesdays and that he drops what he makes there in the evening.

  **Harlan Dorsett** (`ENT-000317`, first written as Dorrit Halvard): Sprint again (Mana 3) to Harrisburg Boulevard; d100 for his presence 83 (present above 20). Reads before speaking (Keen Sense 1, Read the Learner 2, Read the Want 3, Read the Hand 10, Appraise 4; rating 10): Flux Sight 57 -> 100 legendary (**unawakened**; a keeper of the book so nobody can name him without naming who else was there); Read the Learner 40 -> 80 success (he cannot trust anyone with the ledger and has no successor); Read the Want 48 -> 88 strong (invisibility, and an **exit**: to hand the ledger to someone who would seal it; he fears a powerful buyer coming for it); Appraise on the ledger 81 -> 100 legendary (eleven years of core provenance, Gate named where known, buyers in a private shorthand); Read the Hand 73 -> 100 legendary (no techniques, no Mana). He asked one question, whether the person who passed his name still stood well with the protagonist, and was told yes.

  **The exchange.** Told *your name came to me with unlicensed artificers' work* and *not here on official business*, he described the shape of an arrangement in which makers are kept, not employed, and said he had lost two makers who stopped coming, one a woman, on a Tuesday; he keeps a Tuesday buyer he will not name. Asked for her name, the player gave **Marnie**; Dorsett had no such name but had a buyer recorded **M**, cash, A-Rank cores every few weeks by courier to **Fulton Market Parcel & Post, West Fulton Market, Chicago, Box 4126**, last entry a Tuesday in the second week of August then *did not collect*. **This fits Marnie (`ENT-000189`) and does not confirm it or say what happened to her; the concealed-discovery record stays unrevealed.** The player named **CONSULT** aloud; Dorsett had never heard it, only shapes and initials. Asked what he feared: a few weeks ago the Tuesday man asked him, in passing, how he keeps his records. **The Tuesday man collects at 09:00 Chicago on Tuesdays at the unit's side door: a grey coat, a different vehicle each time, no name; he takes the piece and hands back an envelope.** A first draft had Dorsett ask what happens to the ledger as though it had been raised and was re-narrated. **`OBJ-80` opened** (the Tuesday pickup, 2026-09-29 09:00 Chicago, at his own request)."
```

Reads (Keen Sense 1; Flux Sight on her 67 -> 100 legendary and on the office security 59 -> 100 legendary, net +3): she is **unawakened**; the reader, cameras and call panel work; **the rear service door still has the previous tenant's lock** (the guild's is fitted Wednesday 2026-09-30). **The London tunnel is deferred** until that lock is in; **Imogen owes Detroit's front office a call the moment it is fitted.** Anchored step to Amsterdam (MP -5, rift-step 211; **Noor Vandermeulen**), Sprint (Mana 3) 1,130 km to Stockholm in about 80 minutes: **Stockholm anchor set**, **Linnea Bergström** (`ENT-000319`, day manager; first written as Linnea Bergström-Achebe). Reads: Flux Sight on her 32 -> 92 strong (**unawakened**, walks every lock by hand each evening); on the security **99 (a critical) -> 100** (everything works, the guild's own locks throughout). **Gunnar Ekström** (`ENT-000320`, night officer, retired Stockholm police, 26 years; first written as Gunnar Lindqvist-Osei) fitted **an unauthorised rear-door camera on 2026-09-26 at his own expense**, footage on a small drive in the desk, covering the neighbour's lot. **Ruled: nothing outside the secured server**; he may still review on shift; the drive goes; the guild repays his receipt; no paper copy remains. **Linnea owes by 23:00 Stockholm (16:00 Chicago) tonight:** drive removed, camera on the secured server, receipt to Detroit, the paper handover note destroyed after its fact is typed into the front-office record; and HQ IT is asked to mask the neighbour's lot. The Stockholm tunnel is deferred until this is resolved. Anchored step to Seattle (MP -5, rift-step 212).

  Anchors: **62 plus 14 tunnels = 76 of 135.**"

Prose-tracked, no tracked counter: Appraise 2 -> **5**, Read the Learner 3 -> **7**, Read the Want 7 -> **11**, Read the Hand 0 -> **1**. Wrought Hand is still unused.

  **`gatefall.skill_credit`: none. `gatefall.skill_formation`: none;** no dangerous scene and no sealed structured-practice scene; the reads were ordinary uses with no qualifying scene, and twelve taught candidates stand at 1/3. Gold **356,898,131** unchanged; cash $697,398,490.22 -> **$701,358,490.22**: **Tulsa ($1,650,000) and Karlín ($2,310,000) landed** (both at their estimates, Tulsa the next business day and Karlín by 17:00 Chicago), leaving one payout pending, the S-Rank auction lot. Anchors **62 plus 14 tunnels = 76 of 135**."
