description: "**Offered Dario the guild** (I need good people, and I'm the only S-Rank combatant in it); he named a Fremont, California confirmed Gate, carded B, that read at his own Rank or above — he entered solo, walked out, told no one, carried the shame a month. Asked what he'd be: **made senior combat lead on the spot**, and told the standing board-seat offer has no expiration; he accepted the lead immediately and asked the seat go through the board properly rather than be handed to him — Alexander agreed to call a meeting. Told the guild's tunnel network covers members' personal travel too, as guild master's own ruling; Dario named intending to visit his mother in Sacramento. Family established in conversation: mother in Sacramento (occasional contact), father deceased since Dario was 22 (before his own awakening), a married sister in Portland with two children he barely knows; no family in the Bay Area. His BGM history: independent West Coast incident response on the same standing terms Alexander holds, a solo A-Rank clear in Vallejo about eight months prior that nearly went wrong (wary of small enclosed Gates since), no crew, no one to call after a bad clear.

  **Dario formally registered** (guild member, senior combat lead). Arranged: a beer night at the Winnetka house, **Tuesday 2026-09-29, 19:00 Chicago**, Dario bringing beer from a bar in Oakland; whether he stays over is undecided on his side.

  **The San Francisco recruitment event (`OBJ-60`, Day 7's second stop), 17:00-18:05 Pacific.** Worked the crowd by hand, same approach as every stop. **Turnout 700** (500 + 100 x d8 2); **signing rate 28%** (22 + d12 6); **196 signed**: E 65 - D 54 - C 41 - B 29 - A 7 - S 0. **Dario announced publicly** near the event's close as the guild's new S-Rank senior combat lead, to applause and phone cameras; a **late surge** of stragglers reacting to the announcement added **13 more** (d20+10, rolled against the same split): E 2 - D 6 - C 4 - B 0 - A 1 - S 0. **Total signed: 209** (E 67 - D 60 - C 45 - B 29 - A 8 - S 0). **Benjamin Tran** (`ENT-000314`) held the press hold at Alexander's instruction and confirmed the 209 count to HQ; no outlet has Dario's name confirmed, only what phones in the room already captured.

  **Reconciliation, corrected at this save.** A mid-session error briefly double-counted Elias Kane as a second S-Rank member alongside Dario — Kane is guild co-founder and second-in-command, never run through the recruitment tally, and the member Rank line has read S:0 since founding for exactly that reason (`OBJ-60`, `EVT-001129`'s own note). The correction stands: **member S column counts only Dario going forward**, not Kane."
```

Tel Aviv, Istanbul, Stockholm (opened today) and London (no local bench, event not yet run) carry additional gaps.

  **Noa Shapira confirmed as Tel Aviv's day manager**; Gunnar Ekström reconfirmed as Stockholm's night officer. **Seventeen new night officers named and collision-checked** (`-StrictSurnames`), one per office previously covered only by the standing 24/7 policy rather than a named person: Marcus Feldspar (Chicago, `ENT-000325`), Dana Whitcross (Washington DC, `ENT-000326`), Pavel Novy (Prague, `ENT-000327`), Stefan Wendt (Frankfurt, `ENT-000328`), Rosa Delfino (New York, `ENT-000329`), Theo Bramfield (Boston, `ENT-000330`), Yvette Solano (Miami, `ENT-000331`), Cole Ashworth (Houston, `ENT-000332`), Julien Moreau (Paris, `ENT-000333`), Bram Voskuijlen (Amsterdam, `ENT-000334`), Colby Fenwick (Dallas, `ENT-000335`), Priya Sandal (Los Angeles, `ENT-000336`), Ronan Blakely (Seattle, `ENT-000337`), Marisol Ontario (San Francisco, `ENT-000338`), Tamar Golan (Tel Aviv, `ENT-000339`), Baran Yilmaz (Istanbul, `ENT-000340`), Nigel Harcourt (London, `ENT-000341`).

I don't trust her.' Kane declined to act without cause; given one on the second pass — not personnel, a tunnel-mechanism exposure concern for a manager two meetings into the role — accepted it as legitimate and took over executing it (pulling her tunnel-room authorization, searching for a replacement candidate; not yet done, nothing said to her). **Walked back to the venue on foot** once Kane confirmed he'd handle it — no anchor spent; Imogen still running the door, professional, no further comment on the earlier exchange."
```

What followed stayed off-page at the player's own framing; the three of them ended up in bed together. No pool cost; no roll; a purely social/relationship Event."

description: "**The 63rd daily quest lapsed, never attempted before its 23:59 2026-09-29 deadline** — streak **52 -> 0**, first miss since 2026-08-07. The 64th issues fresh at 06:00, same instant.

  **The 06:00 world tick runs.** Chicago: 🎲d4(2)+5 = **7 Gates**. Ranks (🎲 rank rolls): E(42), E(48), C(81), C(87), E(7), D(73), E(8). Siting: 46(elsewhere), 17(within reach), **1(ON HIS ROUTE)**, 59(elsewhere), 98(elsewhere), 40(elsewhere), 63(elsewhere). Rarity: the within-reach E rolls 🎲97 — anomalous, nature unrevealed. **`quiet_days` stood at 2 going in, owing a guaranteed on-route audition** (Section 8.4.7) — satisfied naturally by the C-Rank gate already sited there; card reads C, true Rank and anomaly deferred to first entry (`OBJ-82`). Contest on the within-reach E: 🎲58 vs 15% — not taken, posts **`GB-60`**.

  Prague: 🎲d2(2)+2 = **4 Gates**. Ranks: B(96), E(10), C(85), E(49). Siting: 7(ON ROUTE — surfaces via his own Prague contacts), 20(within reach), 16(within reach), 78(elsewhere, rarity 91 — anomalous). Contest: within-reach E 🎲5 vs 15% — **taken by the trade off-screen**, posts and clears same tick, **`GB-61`**. Within-reach C 🎲92 vs 55% — not taken, posts **`GB-62`**.

  Non-Gate: 🎲51 -> regulatory action, sited elsewhere (🎲57), absorbed off-screen. Agendas: 🎲16 -> Kesha Morrison initiates (minor, unprompted text, nothing urgent). Wider map: 🎲53 -> holds, none.

  No break this tick, no district-pressure change. Escalation credit holds at 11, Tier VII unchanged. `quiet_days` **2 -> 3** — no contested scene of Alexander's own on 09-29 (GB-58/GB-59 were the guild's ordinary dispatch, not his scene). Tomorrow's tick owes a guaranteed on-route audition AND a sited within-reach crisis. Not the week's first tick (turns Thursday 10-01)."

Disposition authored this span (first in-person scene, Decision 092): **want** — to run the one part of this whole operation that never has to be explained twice; **fear** — being handed scope creep with no matching authority, the way Anjali's own expanding list keeps landing partly on his desk; **secret** — he already over-ordered on security hardware in the first week, expecting the roster to double faster than it has, and hasn't corrected the budget line because nobody's asked.

  **Canvassed HQ staff and off-duty hunters informally**, several distinct voices: two Chicago-transfer strikers (gear-backlog complaint, a shared-closet locker-room request); an IT contractor (wifi gaps in the basement server room and stairwells); a reserved sensor-type hunter (a request for a quiet decompression room, no fluorescent lights); a mender in passing (a supply-inventory discrepancy, small items going unlogged). **Texted Cole** (stairwell router coverage, actioned same-day) and **Anjali, cc'd** (a soundproofed quiet-zone lounge with real chairs on the cafeteria floor, lockers/changing rooms ground floor, medical-supply restock plus an automatic reorder threshold) — all folded into her existing list."
```

## EVT-001169 - The Far Door Opened, and Orenne's Slates

```yaml
id: EVT-001169
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-09-30T10:24:00-05:00"
  real_date: "2026-09-24"
type: Event
kind: scene
importance: major
game_date: "2026-09-30T10:17:00-05:00 to 10:24:00-05:00, the Denver facility and the Long Shelf"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000261
counter_deltas:
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.rift_step.successful_uses, delta: 1 }
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.keen_sense.successful_uses, delta: 1 }
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.keen_sense.qualifying_scenes_total, delta: 1 }
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.flux_sight.successful_uses, delta: 1 }

  - subject: ENT-000125

participation_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    result: record-updated
    record: REC-000074
  - subject: ENT-000261
    result: record-updated
    record: REC-000077
description: "**10:17-10:24 Chicago.** **The far door opened at the Denver facility for 2,500 Mana** (Section 18.13.1): 6,562 -> 4,062. Onto the Landing, then an **anchored step to Orenne's ground** (anchor 44, 5 Mana, 4,057). Far-side drain 10% of maximum an hour, no recovery (Section 5.2.1). **Notice +3 (a door onto the Landing), 100 -> 103; the 102 threshold is satisfied by this visit's own contact with Orenne** (the `EVT-001115` precedent).

  **Reads (standing preference 19), each rolled, net +1** (Perception margin +1, the skill's own step or long familiarity +1, Orenne's unreadable composure -1): **Keen Sense** 28 -> 48, partial (1 Mana; the ground clear, figures among the shelters unresolved; +1 detection live for the scene). **Flux Sight** with The Unmeasured 94 -> 100, legendary (Str 3,400 / Agi 2,350 / Vit 1,150 / Per 2,100 / Int 3,050; Health 4,600/4,600, Mana 6,100/6,100; ceiling reached long ago). **Read the Hand** 35 -> 55, partial (10 Mana; Mana full; One Line, Refined and The Count resolved, the other nineteen not separated). **Read the Want** 69 -> 89, strong (3 Mana; he wants the slates read and fears what will be found). **Read the Learner** 90 -> 100, legendary (2 Mana; he is bad at undoing — an age of recording everything once and permanently, no practice at taking a mark back out). **Appraise** on the slates 85 -> 100, legendary (4 Mana): flat stone cut from the Shelf and chiselled by his own hand, all of it within the three days since the last visit; worlds in the order they were examined, each scored pass or fail; **one row near the newest end scraped smooth and re-marked twice, the second yesterday; nothing added to any slate since.** Mana 4,037 after the reads; drain to 10:20 -21 (4,016), to 10:23 -33 (3,983). The reads were ordinary uses on a present subject, not a qualifying scene (the `EVT-001139` precedent; an Appraise mastery crossing narrated in play is withdrawn at the barrier, `EVT-001179`).

  An unrolled observation (Keen Sense scene-live, natural 83 -> 100): stone dust ground to the elbow, a split thumbnail, three days without rest, eyes returning to the scraped row and pulled away; glad Alexander came, and more frightened than he has ever let show. **Orenne** gave the gap as he always does — *two days, twenty-three hours, forty-two minutes* — said he had never written the count down before, there had been no one to show it to, and asked Alexander to read it. Asked whether he came out with anything: **no** — three worlds in the last two centuries he is no longer certain he read correctly; the count itself stands; *a crack*, and he does not know yet whether it is in the wall or in him. **Alexander asked him to come through the door and see Earth.** Orenne did not refuse: *you're asking me to reconsider whether I've earned the right to have made it at all.*"
```

Drain through arrival: **3,945**. **Far door closed at 10:27**, nothing standing in it, **2,500 Mana -> 1,445**. **Notice:** presence 10:17-10:26 and door-open 10:17-10:27 accrue about +0.3 at Section 4.3's hourly rates; the counter reads about 103.3, recorded as **103**. Alexander's far-side drain ends and Conduit's continuous Earth recovery (35% an hour, active) resumes. **Orenne's own drain begins on arrival** under the `EVT-001170` ruling (610 an hour). He put a palm flat to the wall — *it doesn't hum. I expected it to hum* — and asked where he was. Alexander: one of his buildings, and he wanted to show him the cities. Orenne accepted on one condition: *if I ask you to stop, stop.*"

## EVT-001172 - The Cities: Prague, London, Chicago, Tel Aviv, Abisko

```yaml

  - subject: ENT-000301

description: "**10:28-12:14 Chicago.** Five anchored steps (5 Mana each) and one Sprint, Orenne carried each time.

  **Prague**, the café anchor off Wenceslas Square (10:28 Chicago / 17:28 local): a Baroque side street, laundry, a tram bell, bread. *It's still standing. Nobody built this to survive an ending.* — and, the courtesy gone for the first time, *keep going. Please.* **London**, the office anchor (10:29 / 16:29 local): **Imogen Hartley** (`ENT-000318`) at the desk, composed — *Mr. Pendragon. Is there anything the office needs to do?* — told they were passing through, and went back to her screen. The street, Big Ben's half hour. Orenne: *they don't know. Any of them.* **Chicago**, the office anchor (10:30): **Imani Whitaker** (`ENT-000301`) nodded, no line; eight minutes' walk east to **North Avenue Beach** (10:38). *This one's yours, isn't it* — a palm flat to the sand — *this is the part of your case I didn't have an answer prepared for.* Asked if he wanted more: yes, while he had room to hold it; he could feel his pool falling, hours not minutes, and he had noticed Alexander not answering his questions. **Tel Aviv**, the office anchor (10:42 / 18:42 local): **Noa Shapira** (`ENT-000324`), *Mr. Pendragon*, a nod; the walk down to the promenade, Jaffa's stone to the south and the white towers to the north. *I keep waiting for one of them to make my case for me. None of them have yet.* **Stockholm**, the office anchor (10:44 / 17:44 local): **Linnea Bergström** (`ENT-000319`) nodded, no line. **Sprint** (3 Mana, 850 km/h) north about 1,275 km to a ridge above **Abisko**, Swedish Lapland — ninety minutes, arriving 12:14 / 19:14 local. Orenne laughed, the first time Alexander has heard it: *the fastest I have ever moved in my life.* Asked whether any of it changed anything: **the count did not move — beauty was never the argument; what moved is him**, and the arithmetic can no longer be done from a distance. Alexander: *maybe*, to whether he had simply wanted him to see it.

  **Sprint here was ordinary traversal: a use, not a qualifying scene** (the `EVT-000957` precedent; play narrated a qualifying scene, withdrawn at `EVT-001179`). **Mana, corrected at the barrier:** play treated Conduit's recovery as negligible through the tour and then booked a single +3,445 for the Sprint leg (a narrated 4,862 at Abisko); the continuous 35% an hour (about 38 a minute) actually ran from 10:27 and put him near **5,500** at 12:14. Orenne, 610 an hour since 10:26: about **5,000 / 6,100** at Abisko."
```

Orenne agreed, restating that nothing about the count moves because of it, and closed the distance himself. What followed was mutual and left off-page; he kept his eyes open throughout, one hand flat on Alexander's chest — *I wanted to remember that one properly.* (An earlier narration that skipped ninety minutes here was withdrawn at the player's direction; nothing from it stands.)

  **Then Rupture, unannounced, point-blank, at a target with no readiness.** **Pre-empt** (S-Rank grant: first blade resolves before any opponent at any Rank). Natural **93**, +3 steps (capped: point-blank, total surprise, zero readiness) -> **100, legendary**; about **12,773** damage against Health **4,600**. **Orenne (`ENT-000261`) killed**, his hand still on Alexander's chest. 10 Mana. No exchange for Orenne existed. Uncontested — Section 7.4's contest test fails (no real resolution against him, no cost on failure) — so a use and no qualifying scene; Rupture stands at Master."
```

    record: REC-000074
description: "**Succession fired** (Profile Section 7.6.2, rung two: a System-bearer, the Bearer's own damage crossing zero, the death real). Section 7.6.2 stops wherever a champion's entry authors no mastery; the owner answered it and the rest, out of character:

  1. **Every technique taken arrives at S-Rank and Master** — the entries `270_THE_FAR_SIDE.md` authors at A, B and C included.
  2. **Declined** under Section 7.6.4: **Never Raised** and **Ninety-One Worlds** (each binds the holder's own choice), and **The Doubt** (no authored effect). Not acquired, not banked.
  3. **The six he already held from Orenne's teaching** — The Count, Tally, Weight of Precedent, Arithmetic, Patience of the Ledger, Nothing Is Wasted, each Novice, taught, `transmissible: true` — **rise to Master**, by owner ruling at the barrier, overriding Section 7.6.3's *already held, nothing taken*. They stay taught and transmissible. (The Succession window shown in play listed all seventeen as transcribed; six were already held. Corrected at the barrier.)
  4. **Eleven transcribe new**, `acquisition_route: succession`, `transmissible: false`, `taken_from: ENT-000261`: One Line, Refined; Recount; Closing Argument; The Clean Failure; Let It Finish; The Argument; Attrition; Course of Nature; Unhurried; Ledgerhand; What He Is Trying to Make Him Do. Six of them were still tracking as taught candidates at 1/3 (Recount, Closing Argument, The Clean Failure, Let It Finish, The Argument, Attrition); those candidates close as superseded.
  5. **Nothing Is Wasted's scope widens to every skill he holds**: any failed activation refunds its Mana; the miss still misses.
  6. **A champion killed on Earth is outside the Long Shelf's channel.** Section 4.3's notice keys on presence in the Stacks, and Section 4.4's *they all learn of a death* was written for a death where the population is; nobody there perceives this one. **No +12 notice, no hardening of any position, no Tolo ruling owed** — Aumry and Ilith still teach, Sevrin's condition stands. (Play first applied the +12 and the hardening; both withdrawn on this ruling.)

  Section 18.13.1 pays nothing and no Kill XP row exists for a bearer; no XP. **Unhurried** was questioned by the player as a downgrade: it is not — it governs exchanges he does not open, Pre-empt those he does."
```

Fuel from the dimensional inventory — a quantity never itemised on the sheet, recorded here as a fill and consumed — and he was burned where he lay, in the room the door had opened into. An ordinary fire does not reach cremation temperature, and with Rupture's Unmaking damage what remains is **ash, char and shattered bone fragments**. Gathered into a lidded metal container and **stored in the dimensional inventory**. One corner of the room scorched. Orenne was never licensed, carded, or on any Earth record; no Earth authority has a channel to any of it. *Farewell, Orenne.* Mana full by about 12:42 (Conduit). (A first narration moved the body to an invented incinerator on a maintenance level and attached Detroit's head of security to the Denver site; withdrawn at the player's direction.)"
```

  **Owner ruling on the A-Rank registry** (the `EVT-001134` registry was read at 2,817 members and never counted sensors): of the **192 A-Rank cards**, **12 menders, 12 artificers, 14 sensors — and sensors are combat-capable**. Under the standing memo (`EVT-001109`, restated `EVT-001129`: seventeen strikers per A-Rank crew, every one A-Rank, with a mender and a sensor outside the count and support to the posting floor), that leaves 168 who can fight, **154 strikers, and nine standing A-Rank crews** — 153 strikers on the line and one spare; a sensor on every crew and five in reserve who can fill the line; a mender on every crew and three in reserve. description: "**14:24-14:31.** Asked how the regulators stood, Anjali said it before being asked twice: **no filing accepted in writing.** The German and French authorities' verbal *end of day 09-29* was not kept (recorded in play as *Region III*, a mislabel from `EVT-001149`) -- the 09-30 06:00 tick had already recorded no branch operational, and whether its written acceptance had landed since was rolled (**d100 35 against 51+: no**). She had chased it that morning; the clerk wanted certified credentials for each office head, sent by Anselm and Odile by about 11:00 Chicago. **Both now owe a decision by Thu 10-01 12:00 Frankfurt (05:00 Chicago).** Region V and National's provisional-dispatch question: nothing new to her; both are Kane's (`EVT-001160`). Her own-initiative beat: Milwaukee and Toledo are the one open door (`EVT-001132`'s ruling).

  **Alexander: take both, staff from Chicago, and it should not wait for his approval.** **Standing instruction:** HQ dispatch confirms and staffs contract-town tickets on its own authority; into the standing memo tonight. Confirmation calls to Toledo's city manager and Milwaukee emergency management inside their four-hour windows. **Five Gates dispatched under the standing team composition** (`151_HUNTER_POPULATION_MODEL.md` Section 6: strikers at the team's Rank -- 5 at D and C, 9 at B -- one mender and one sensor outside the count; **C, B and A teams bring a mining team, E and D teams mine the Gate themselves** -- owner ruling, 2026-09-24 real date): Toledo rail-yard **B** (9 strikers, 16 in all), Toledo port **C** (5, 11 in all), Toledo downtown **D** (5, 7 in all), Milwaukee Menomonee Valley **C** (5, 11 in all), Milwaukee Jones Island **D** (5, 7 in all). Chicago fills first, Detroit tops up nearest-outward without asking. Milwaukee drives up from the Chicago office; Toledo comes through the HQ tunnel and an hour's road south.

  **Authored this Event:** Milwaukee's two tickets are a **C in the Menomonee Valley** and a **D on Jones Island** (the record said only *two*); Toledo's rail-yard B was **detected Sun 09-27 22:00 Eastern**, breaking **Thu 10-01 21:00 Chicago** on Section 9.3's four-day timer. *(Corrections at the player's word: the first narration dropped the miners from all five rosters, and put one striker on each D; D teams run five strikers like C (Section 6), and every crew went in with its miners. Owner ruling after the save: E and D teams carry no extra miners and mine the Gate themselves; C, B and A teams always bring a mining team.)*"
```

Mana 3+2+10+4 = 19.

  *20 minutes max, on a different matter.* In the interview room he laid out 07:42 on 09-21 from memory (the $340,000 certified instrument carding E; Renata's hold; the drone confirming S; the clear at 08:37) and asked **what Alexander saw**, not how. **Disclosed, by Alexander's own words:** he read the Gate as S from outside the mouth, at a run, nothing concealed, the same way he knows Boyce is D-Rank with a pool of 50. Boyce's conclusion: the error is the instrument and the carding -- **Region V cards its E and D Gates from mast-mounted remote instruments with no sensor sent to check at the tape** (authored). **Alexander signed a one-paragraph witness statement** for the review: read as S from outside, unaided, in passing, communicated to Renata before entry, nothing concealed or unusual. Boyce kept it on his person, not in the folder. He offered to walk Alexander wherever he was going. No channel to Flux Sight by name or any System state; what he holds is what was said aloud."

```yaml

description: "**Boyce walked him up to Desmond Farraday.** Record loaded before the first line; no Flux Sight window (read before, unchanged). Reads at +3 (Intelligence margin +2, Keen Sense +1): **Read the Want** 51 -> 100, Legendary -- the Pendragon Guild inside the system on paper he can defend; fears the Director is right about this one; **Read the Learner** 86 -> 100, Legendary -- he names walls and has never climbed one; **Read the Hand** 28 -> 88, Strong -- one minor technique, **Stillwater**, a settling pressure that takes the heat out of a room; 50/50 Mana. Mana 15.

  **The wall, authored as world fact:** Chicago's standing-relationship filing went in **without the dispatch-log annex** every other guild files (real-time reporting of each crew sent into a Region V Gate); intake saw the name and sent it straight to the Director, who read the omission as Alexander's August refusal repeated (`EVT-000684`). Kane had it from Farraday at 09:10. Challenged -- *you couldn't tell us on day one?* -- Farraday owned it: he knew Monday afternoon and waited for Kane. **Counter-proposal, his own:** an **entry notice** (the Gate, crew names, time of crossing) at entry, and **roster and results after clearance**; no live feed, nothing about method. Alexander: bureaucracy excusing an institution, but he would sign -- on sight of their security protocols and GDPR; nothing unencrypted. Farraday named the wall plainly: **GDPR does not bind a US federal agency; the Privacy Act and federal information-security standards do.** Released three documents: the secure intake portal specification (encrypted in transit and at rest, named-user access logged, no email path), the dispatch-data records notice (readable by Ranking, Dispatch, the cordon desk, and Incident Review on a flagged incident; no outside party short of a court order), and the retention schedule (one year for every entry record); the internal security manual is never released. Terms sheet written by hand with a **requested 90-day retention on clean clears**; Alexander read all and **signed for the Guild**. The Director would see him at 15:15. Path stated by Farraday: acceptance tonight puts Chicago on the 06:00 rotation."
```

Reads at +3 (Intelligence margin +2 against C-Rank 30, Keen Sense +1), all Legendary: **Read the Hand** (44) -- Still Room only, run within the hour for a Washington call (29 Mana); **Read the Want** (72) -- to leave the chair clean, and here, the relationship signed on terms no other guild can lever; fear: that the lakefront finding lands on the instrument rather than an operator, which she already knows it will; **Read the Learner** (71) -- cannot hold a fact she cannot manage. Mana 15.

At the door, passive voice: the Region *would find it helpful* if the lakefront review concluded on its merits without undue public attention. Alexander said goodbye and stepped home from her office."

## EVT-001188 - Five Contract Gates, Cleared by the Guild

```yaml
id: EVT-001188
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.8"
status: active

  source: session-gameplay

  - subject: ENT-000288
    result: record-updated
    record: REC-000077
description: "**The guild's first contract-town clears, reported by Anjali as each crew crossed and came out.** Crossings: Milwaukee Menomonee C 16:39; Milwaukee Jones Island D 16:51; Toledo rail-yard B 17:05; Toledo port C 17:13; Toledo downtown D 17:21. **One roll per clear, each crew at +2** (+1 for the extra striker in every team, +1 for a mender and a sensor):

  - **Jones Island D:** 16 -> 56, Partial. Cleared, out 17:50; one of the five strikers' forearm broken on the boss's last swing, set by the mender, not closed tonight -- urgent care, off rotation two days.
  - **Menomonee C:** 75 -> 100, Legendary. Out 18:02, clean and fast.
  - **Toledo downtown D:** 1 -> 41, Partial. Out 18:17; one striker concussed with two ribs broken, finishing the boss from the floor, overnight in hospital in Toledo for observation, expected fine.
  - **Toledo port C:** 57 -> 97, Exceptional. Out 18:34, clean; the sensor marked a crystal vein in the second chamber, which the crew's miners cut before they came out.
  - **Toledo rail-yard B:** 96 -> 100, Legendary. Out 18:47, no injuries, twelve minutes under plan; **the break due Thu 10-01 21:00 is averted.** Toledo's city manager rang HQ to thank the guild in person.

  **Every Gate was mined** -- the C's and the B by their mining teams, the D's by their own crews; yields are in each crew's HQ results log and are not itemised here. All five results reached Region V (Anjali by hand; the connector went live at 18:06 and filed the B's results automatically). **Two hurt, nobody lost.** Anjali proposed adding a striker to D teams; **Alexander: D is scaled like C and five clears are not a sample -- wait until at least two offices run Gates regularly under regulator approval.** She will pull injury rate by Rank from the rotation onward and change nothing until then. Contract payment owed to the guild by the two cities under `EVT-001132`'s arrangements; not yet received."
```

Keen Sense, scene (natural 90, +3 -> 100, Legendary; 1 Mana). **Read the Want** at +3 (Intelligence margin +2 against S-Rank 60, Keen Sense +1): **Kane** 37 -> 97, Exceptional -- wants to hand over the Region V answer he spent the day on and have it be the useful thing; fears it was handled without him; **Dario** 4 -> 64, Success -- wants to show the training announcement; fears nobody comes Saturday. Mana 7. Gym shorts and a training shirt drawn from the pocket dimension in front of both (**a channel opens for Kane and Dario: the witnessed withdrawal itself**, nothing of mechanism).

  Kane gave the annex answer he owed; told Alexander had gone to the Director herself, he said plainly *I wanted to be the one who brought you that*, and on Alexander's *you could have told me* owned it -- he meant to bring a done thing rather than another problem; next time the problem when he gets it. Told everything that happened at Region V, he named the risk: **Okafor now knows a statement exists and who holds it; two pages already left Boyce's folder on 09-24.** Alexander: BGM is boring; tell me about you two.

  **Training, rebuilt.** Dario's 13:40 post had drawn **206 Chicago sign-ups and about forty from other offices in five hours**. Alexander: use the finished outdoor yard at HQ (`EVT-001164`); then better -- **the nine A-Rank commanders first, trained by Dario; then open sessions spread over three or four days; once the training floor and yard are finished, every commander runs one lesson a week for the Ranks below and Dario moves up to the A-Ranks; the S-Rank network on a rota -- all of whom already promised it; and Kane on it too.** Dario: commanders **Saturday 10-03** at HQ; open sessions Tuesday to Friday next week, about sixty a day; Cole told first. He was never sent the commander list; Kane: *you'll have it tonight*. Pembroke is in London, untunnelled -- Alexander: no plane tickets; London is on his schedule tomorrow. Menders' combat track: **Thursday 10-01 18:00 Eastern (17:00 Chicago)** at HQ, mats in the big conference room; Doc Haldane coming to heckle. Kane, asked, said yes. Food on the guild's salaries: Dario ordered three deep dish, ETA 20:10."
```

Against the guild's artificers (`151_HUNTER_POPULATION_MODEL.md` Section 5: E 94, D 74, C 54, B 32, A 12 -- 266; play first used the stale `EVT-001134` read, 176, and is corrected here), at a planning figure of 2/2/3/5/7 days a piece (Section 12.8 says only *days* at E-C): E 2 days, D 8, C 12, B 15, A about 4 weeks.

  **Alexander: E and D wait on the guild's artificers; buy three quarters of C and B at the Section 12.8.1 licensed anchor: 176 C x $12,500 = $2,200,000 and 58 B x $55,000 = $3,190,000, about $5.39M of guild money**, C tonight and B as the licensed desks open; the rest forged in-house, and the outlay recovered afterwards by the artificers forging for sale. **A-Ranks held**; he will tell Anjali tomorrow whether something he means to try has worked. **B and C artificers forge down to D as their own queues clear**: after the purchases 58 C and 19 B remain, so 50 C artificers are free from day 3 and 13 B artificers from day 0; D (74 artificers, 37 a day) rises to about 68 a day and finishes about day 5; the last C's land about day 6; **everyone below A kitted by about Wed 10-07.** (Play said Thu 10-08 on the stale counts.) Cores assumed in stock; Anjali checks tomorrow.

  **Dinner and a film.** Three deep dish (Dario converted by the third slice); a heist film of Dario's choosing. Kane's phone buzzed once at **21:48**; he read it screen-away, went still for a second, pocketed it and said nothing (Keen Sense caught it; nothing was asked). The three of them on the sofa; Dario asleep by the credits at 22:34. Kane kept the clock -- the fence at four. **Travelling rift-step, close band, to the master bedroom with both of them touching him** (3 Mana; Riftwalker's S rung, *an ally he touches may be sent with him*, read as each ally touched -- flagged for the owner). Beer closes at five Urquell and six Oakland."
```

description: "**The 64th daily lapsed incomplete at 00:00** (streak 0 -> 0). Before the skip the Runtime named the deadline; the player chose the penalty zone. **Transfer at 00:00: a sealed survival instance at Combat Tier S** (Section 6.7: no Rank's common Health exceeds his best standard strike). Rolled: 3d6 = 5+5+3 = 13; 1d2 elites = 2; archetype d8 = 2, **Hive** -- commons doubled and the elite slots made commons: **28 Hive Drones [S] (4,000/2,000 each) and the Hive Queen [S] (16,000/2,000)**; Cartographer knew it at the threshold. Kit worn from the pocket dimension before the second heartbeat.

  **Fifteen exchanges, each an in-scene rift-step (1 Mana, +1) and a split dual strike (no Mana), one knife a drone, at +3 (Agility margin +2 against 60, rift +1).** Strike naturals: 73 83 | 19 32 | 4 5 (both fumbles) | 8 81 | 43 100 | 44 68 | 28 42 | 84 44 | 39 99 | 92 48 | 16 100 | 90 98 | 13 21 | 70 91 | 99 36. Every landed strike (5,526 against 4,000) killed. **Drones answering, evasion at +2**, naturals: 70 40 82 | 53 23 99 | 58 87 6 (Guard Echo, 66) | 19 70 65 | 98 57 37 | 73 2 12 | 6 96 60 | 12 11 78 | 82 17 66 | 16 18 49 | 8 73 79 | 88 44 98 | 61 13 60 | 58 25. Ten grazes at 49 and one fumble at 97, against total reduction 90.28% (armour 89.2%, Loner 10% alone): **587 Health.** **The Queen:** a sixteenth in-scene step, then **Rupture** (10 Mana): natural 21 -> 81, Strong x2; (1,000 + 3,282) x 3.05 x 2 x 1.15 (Giantslayer) x 1.10 (Godslayer) = 33,042 against 16,000 (effective Intelligence 3,282 after the level-208 rung), Unmaking through the carapace. Her attack as he closed evaded (36 -> 76). The instance collapsed at about 00:03.

  **XP (Section 3.3, no underdog bonus at S):** 28 x 1,000 = 28,000; the Queen 1,000 x 4 x 2 (Godslayer) = 8,000; S-Rank clear milestone 3,200; **+39,200.** 6,642 + 39,200 = 45,842: **Level 207 -> 208** at the fifteenth drone (exchange 9), **208 -> 209** at the Queen, spending 20,700 and 20,800; **4,342/20,900.** Each level: +1 every base Stat (past level 100), +5 unspent, restoration. Base Strength 2,931, Agility 2,656, Vitality 2,948, Perception 2,657, Intelligence 3,262; effective Vitality 2,961, Agility 2,711, Strength 2,967, Intelligence 3,283; **Health 11,844/11,844, Mana 6,566/6,566** after the second restoration; **unspent 10.** No loot, by rule. No title assessment (209). **Contest:** nothing in the instance could beat him (97 at most a hit against 11,844), so no combat skill earns a qualifying scene (Section 7.4); uses only. He came back in clean clothes -- the kit stored -- to Kane awake in the dark: *You went.* *To pee.*"
```

description: "**Promotion barrier for `EVT-001180`-`EVT-001193`, at the player's `/save`.** Closing instant 2026-10-01 03:32 Chicago, the Winnetka house.

  **Audit matrix.** Entering (`EVT-001179`): Health 11,836/11,836, Mana 6,562/6,562, both resting, remainders 0. `EVT-001180`-`001181` 14:22-14:31: no cost. `EVT-001182` 14:32: anchored step -5, Keen Sense -1 (6,556); mode change to active/light; Conduit S active 40% = 80 rate units, about 43.7 Mana a minute: full again by about 14:33. `EVT-001183` 14:34: -19 (6,543), full by about 14:36. `EVT-001184` about 14:48: -15, full by about 14:49. `EVT-001185` 15:16: -15, full by about 15:17. `EVT-001186` 15:24: anchored step -5, full within the minute; resting from 15:31. `EVT-001187`-`001189`: no cost. `EVT-001190` 18:55: Keen Sense -1, Read the Want x2 -6, full within the minute. `EVT-001191` 22:47: travelling step -3, repaid. `EVT-001192` 00:00-00:03: in-scene steps -16, Rupture -10, Health -587; level 208 restored both to the new maxima (11,840/6,564), level 209 restored both again (**11,844/6,566**). `EVT-001193`: none. **Closing: Health 11,844/11,844, Mana 6,566/6,566, both resting, remainders 0.** (A play-time line at 14:33 gave recovery as under a point; the Conduit-inclusive rate was corrected in play and stands here. Play rendered maximum Health as 11,832 all session, and 11,840 after the Hive; the sheet has carried 11,836 since the Gale Feet re-read of 2026-09-24, so the true figures are 11,836 entering and **11,844** at 209. No resolution turned on the difference.)

  **Counters re-counted from the resolved actions.** `rift_step.successful_uses` 237 -> **256** (anchored to the BGM lobby, anchored home, the travelling bedroom step, sixteen in-scene steps in the Hive; the 238/239 narrated at the Winnetka and Region V steps in play is superseded by this count). `keen_sense.successful_uses` 169 -> **171**, `qualifying_scenes_total` 82 -> **84** (two activations, the `EVT-001168` convention). `flux_sight.successful_uses` 173 -> **177** (the lobby room row, Boyce, Okafor, the Hive). `rupture.successful_uses` 237 -> **238**. `dagger_mastery.successful_uses` 609 -> **623** (fourteen exchanges in which a knife landed; exchange 3 landed nothing). Prose-tracked: Read the Want 13 -> **18**, Read the Learner 8 -> **11**, Read the Hand 2 -> **5**, Appraise 6 -> **7**; per `EVT-001179`, reads are ordinary uses and not qualifying scenes, so no mastery moves. `progression.ability_points_earned` 1,319 -> **1,329** (two level-ups, +10 unspent).

  **World-side commitments settled at this barrier from their owners' own state, unwitnessed:** Director Okafor's letters to the US regional directors went out at 16:40; Kesha's neighbourhood shortlist reached Anjali at 21:10. Opened: eight tunnel rooms by 10-09 and the B purchases and core check by 10-01 17:00 (Anjali); the menders' track 10-01 and the commanders' session 10-03 (Dario).

  **`gatefall.skill_credit`:** the Hive carries its deltas. **`gatefall.skill_formation`: none;** the penalty zone was dangerous by name and uncontested in fact (Section 7.4). Taught candidates unchanged at 1/3. **No pending ratification; no provisional mechanic open.**

  **Reconciled totals.** Level **209**, XP **4,342/20,900**. Unspent **10**, pending Ability Points 4, Status Recovery 45. Daily streak 0; the 64th failed; the 65th issues 06:00. `world_clock` unchanged (last 09-30 06:00, next 10-01 06:00); `quiet_days` 3. Anchors 81 of 135 (cap 2,712 / 20 = 135). Cash and gold unchanged (the guild's $5.39M is guild money, not his). Far-side notice 103.

  **New entity:** Harlan Boyce (`ENT-000352`), whose name was already canon (`OBJ-68`); the collision tool's REVIEW against Harlan Dorsett is a coincidence accepted, not a relation. **Owner questions raised this span:** the 63rd daily's missed penalty zone (09-29/30 midnight, beer night; no transfer fired and none is applied retroactively without a ruling); the reading of Riftwalker's *an ally he touches* for two allies at once; the artificer throughput figures used for planning; **whether E and D teams carry miners** -- ruled after the save: they mine the Gate themselves, with no extra miners. `151`'s Section 7 now records Chicago accepted, the Frankfurt and Paris deadline and London's lock. **Standing preference added:** the per-turn settlement block is shown only on request."
```

Reads at +3 (Intelligence margin +2 against C 30, Keen Sense +1): Flux Sight [C] 250/250, 125/125, ceiling C; **Read the Hand** 51 -> 100 (Stonefoot, a rooted stance, never used in a Gate); **Read the Want** 10 -> 70 (the names read aloud and heard by this man; fears his brother becomes a number); **Read the Learner** 24 -> 84 (cannot let anyone carry anything for him). He had a list of **211 names** from the valley's own towns. Told *it's your decision*, he chose to read them himself. *(Re-narrated at the player's word: a first draft had him place Alexander at the fence like a defendant; he asked instead, and named that Alexander owed them nothing, was not awakened when it began, and ended it.)* Alexander stood beside him. At noon, with the town's bell, Andrei read 211 names in thirteen minutes, his brother **Ionuț Bratu, twenty-six** among them. Mana 15."
```

Ionuț went up the second winter for double pay, his wife pregnant; his daughter is five. Alexander took the photograph and said thank you. **Gheorghe Dincă** (`ENT-000355`, minted), her brother, a miner, unawakened: Read the Want 53 -> 100 (to hear it could have been done sooner; under it, to be told it was not his fault -- he drove Ionuț to the sign-up), Read the Learner 97 -> 100 (argues with the answer he expects). *Where were you?* Alexander: unawakened, living an ordinary life; several S-Ranks died trying; whoever sent low Ranks to hold that fence should answer for their deaths; he ended it because he heard stories like theirs; his guild is built to protect hunters, not spend them. Gheorghe broke and wept against his sister. Andrei: *you gave us a better question: who signed the rotations.* **World fact, settled by the owner in play:** by 2026 the Exclusion held only its apex alpha (`EVT-000905`); the rotations had ground the rest down, mixed crews under A- and S-Rank leads with low Ranks on perimeter, sensors and rescue; they died on the line when the alpha's ground-wave reached their sector. Ionuț died that way. Mana 10."
```

description: "Asked whether the guild would take the valley's young awakened: **of course, anyone.** Five came forward (three E, two D). **Ioana Stoica** (`ENT-000356`, minted), D-Rank, the one who asks: Flux Sight [D] 100/100, 50/50, ceiling D; Read the Hand 23 -> 83 (Spark Line, a thrown mana filament); Read the Want 34 -> 94 (a way off the fence that is not the rotation; fears saying yes to double pay next spring); Read the Learner 21 -> 81 (cannot believe an offer that costs nothing). Alexander: **no office in Romania yet; he will do what is in his power to open one, not tomorrow; meanwhile go to Prague and join.** The clause, asked for and given honestly: they may be sent from office to office working Gates, **never above their Rank**, and may join mining teams instead of fighting. Pay: **a little above the standard US hunter salary; international insurance on the same terms as New York**. The rotation had paid double and then nothing (*contained zone*); Ionuț's daughter got nothing. Ioana: **Sunday night bus from Petroșani, Prague by Monday afternoon, all five.** Viorica took the photograph back: *nobody made that promise to Ionuț.* Gheorghe nodded once. Mana 15."

The embankment four died in exchange 3, the exchange they would have reached the train. Civilian-threat rolls: car 23/6/79 against 81+, bakery 9/83/1 against 91+, police 1/71/76/81 against 86+ -- none reached. **15/15, nobody hurt. Quest complete.** XP: 12 x 60 + 2 x 120 + 240 x 2 (Godslayer) + 6,800 = **+8,240**. Uncontested (Section 7.4): uses only. Mana 30. Weapons recalled after the fight, free. The **Chicago branch** arrived second on its first emergency contract (issued 06:03): **Tamsin Okoro** (`ENT-000357`, minted), C-Rank team lead -- Flux Sight [C] 250/250, 125/125; Read the Hand 87 -> 100 (Crossguard, a parry into her footing); Read the Want 79 -> 100 (the branch answering without the founder; fears arriving second defines her team); Read the Learner 99 -> 100 (cannot take praise). Told genuinely that they were fast; **harvest rights and the papers to the branch** (Alexander waived his kills). Mana 15 for her reads."

National's two messages: **room 7C, seventh floor**, and **the Director asks that Mr. Kane attend**. A third step with both, through the window into **the unheated pool**; Kane held the water still with **Standfast** for the dunk. A fourth into **the boathouse sauna, unlit**; Kane built the fire. **Kane's wet shirt could not be taken off him into the inventory (Section 12.9: nothing worn by a person); he shrugged it off and watched it vanish the instant it left him -- *that's new* -- and asked nothing.** Ten minutes that were twelve; then a travelling step (close band, 3 Mana) to the bedroom with both. Asked directly whether he *wanted* to attend, Kane: *nobody asks me that* -- yes, for himself, and *because it's you going in*; replied *Mr. Kane will attend*. Clothes: Alexander's hoodies to both, Kane kept the favourite joggers. **New York's letter in writing, 11:00.** Dario and Alexander; then Kane joined (faded at the threshold both times, non-explicit at the player's direction). Lunch at 12:24: Tuesday's deep dish, Kane with a fork. Steps: bed 1, bed 1, pool 1, sauna 1 (Mana each), bedroom 3."
```

Pembroke rang: she walks to Saturday and goes through first. **The UK's national authority: 🎲 01, a fumble.** It rang at 16:00 London and **returned the filing as incomplete**: the office head's credentials to be certified (as Frankfurt and Paris), and **a named responsible officer resident in the United Kingdom**; Imogen sent it to Detroit at 16:30; the review clock restarts only on a complete filing. **Alexander: no proxy -- the authority accepts him, or London shuts.** Imogen, once: the letter says *resident*. Seoul Friday, so **Monday**. **Two canvasses for a London lawyer tonight failed** (the lease solicitors' duty line, 26; three City firms, 24; net 0 each); **the lease solicitors' regulatory partner held Mon 10-05, 09:00 London (03:00 Chicago)**, Imogen to confirm tomorrow; two City firms to ring back Friday morning. The case officer is in from 08:00 London. London clock 19:00: the GB-63 lot's auction opened in Washington."
```
